

tamiḻar varalāṟu

iṉṟiya nōkkil

(paṇṭu mutal iṉṟu vari)






pi. irāmanātaṉ ka.mu; ca.i.











tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
ceṉṉi - 600 017.



nūṟ kuṟippu

patippāḷar 			: kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

nūlāciriyariṉ muṉṉuri

tamiḻ, tamiḻariṉ toṉmi, muṉmi, teṉmi ākiyavaṟṟi nilināṭṭuvataṟkāṉa maṟukkoṇāta cāṉṟukaḷ kaṭanta irupatu-muppatu āṇṭukaḷil 

māntiṉat tōṟṟamum paravalum cārnta āyvukaḷ,
māntamarapaṇuviyal;
nāstirātik / yūrēciyāṭik maṟṟum ñāla
mutaṉmoḻi āyvukaḷ,
tolliyal akaḻāyvukaḷ

pōṉṟa pala tuṟikaḷilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi yellām uṭkoṇṭu eḻutappaṭṭa tamiḻar, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu nūl itu. innūliṉ mutal mūṉṟu iyalkaḷil ivvivaraṅkaḷik kāṇalām. āṅkāṅku tēviyāṉa nilappaṭaṅkaḷum vivarappaṭaṅkaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

2. iyal 1il “varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa?” eṉṉum iraṇṭām pakutiyil tokuttuḷḷa pēraṟiñar palariṉ karuttukaḷ innūli maṭṭumalla, enta varalāṟṟu nūlip paṭippavarkaḷum maṉatiṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyavi ākum.

3. caṅka kālam toṭaṅki iṉṟu vari uḷḷa kālattiṉ varalāṟu paṟṟiyavi iyalkaḷ 4-10 ākum. avaṟṟilum putiya ceytikaḷiyum karuttukaḷiyum kāṇalām.

4. āyvāḷarkaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷ karuttukaḷ āṅkāṅkē avarkaḷ coṉṉavāṟē tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. - - paṭippavar naṉmi karuti. mutaṉmiyāṉa karuttukaḷiṉ āṅkila mūlamum tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu (e.kā: tamiḻiṉ cemmoḻic ciṟappu paṟṟiya aṟiñar kūṟṟukaḷ; avi piṟamoḻiyāḷarukkum terivikka vēṇṭiyaviyākaliṉ. eḷitil ōriṭattil kiṭṭāta āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷit tēṭāmal, ipputtakattiliruntē eṭuttut tara vaki ceytuḷḷatu nallatu tāṉē). mutaṉmiyāṉa pārvi nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal antanta iyaliṉ iṟutiyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. annūlkaḷ kaṭṭurikaḷiṉ āciriyarkaḷ aṉivarukkum naṉṟi. avarkaḷuṭiya aṟivāṟṟalum uḻippum iṉṟēl innūl ētu?

5. (i) ēṟkeṉavē eṉatu piṉvarum varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷic civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu:

1998. A new account of the history and culture of the Tamils

1999. cintuveḷit toltamiḻa nākarikam

tamiḻ, tamiḻar varalāṟu paṟṟiya āyvukkaṭṭurikaḷi āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum pala āyvitaḻkaḷil 1969il iruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷēṉ. 1977-81il tēvanēyap pāvāṇar centamiḻccelvi itaḻil veḷiyiṭṭa “tamiḻiṉ talimiyi nāṭṭum coṟkaḷ” eṉṉum kaṭṭurit toṭariṉ (140 pakka) āṅkila moḻipeyarppi Nostratics - the light from Tamil according to Devaneyan eṉatu (60 pakka) muṉṉuriyuṭaṉ kaḻakam 2004il veḷiyiṭṭatu. ivvakiyil eṉṉi ūkkuvitta kaḻaka āṭciyāḷar irā. muttukkumāracāmi avarkaḷukku naṉṟi.

(ii) tamiḻar varalāṟu cārnta piṉvarum nūlkaḷiṉ eṉatu tamiḻākkatti (iṉṟiya niliyik kāṭṭum eṉatu muṉṉurikaḷuṭaṉ updating forward) tamiḻmaṇ patippakattār veḷiyiṭṭu eṉakku ūkkam aḷittaṉar.

1. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar 1947. The origin and spead of the Tamils (tamiḻariṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum 2006).

2. alekcāntar kāntiratāv 1974 The riddles of the three occans nūliṉ iraṇṭām pakuti (intu mākkaṭal) maṭṭum (tamiḻa nākarikamum cintuveḷi nākarikamum. 2006)

3. pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār 1929. History of the Tamils from the earliest times to 600 AD (tamiḻar varalāṟu - ki.pi. 600 vari. 2007) 

“toṉmic cemmoḻi tamiḻ” eṉṉum eṉatu nūliyum tamiḻmaṇ patippakamē 2007-il veḷiyiṭṭatu. ippoḻutu intap puttakattiyum eḻuti veḷiyiṭa muṭintatu tamiḻmaṇ patippaka kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷ eṉṉi iṭiviṭātu ūkkuvittu vantataṉāltāṉ. avarukku eṉṟum naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.

patippuri

tamiḻariṉ varalāṟu taṉicciṟappuḷḷatu; māntar varalāṟṟoṭum moḻi varalāṟṟoṭum intiya varalāṟṟoṭum toṭarpuṭiyatu. palvēṟu tuṟikaḷilum (puviyiyal, māntaviyal, mānta marapaṇuviyal, moḻiyiyal, kumūkaviyal, tolliyal, tolleḻuttiyal, varalāṟu, varalāṟṟu variviyal, araciyal, poruḷātāram, ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu pōṉṟavi) iṉṟu paṉṉāṭṭu nallaṟiñar potuvāka ēṟṟuḷḷa karuttōṭṭaṅkaḷi – ērāḷamāṉa paltuṟikkaruvi nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷic cevvaṉē
uḷvāṅki -- eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa innūliṉ pāṅki ati mēmpōkkākap pārppavarkaḷum uṇarvar. tēviyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil ellām cāṉṟukaḷ antantat tuṟiyiṉ iṉṟiya aṟiñar kūṟiyavāṟē tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iyalkaḷiṉ iṟutiyil ātāra nūṟpaṭṭiyalkaḷ muḻumiyākat tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

2. ituvari ittakiya piṟa nūlkaḷil kāṇappeṟāta karuttukaḷip poṟuttavari āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷum tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa-- paṭippōr naṉmi karutiyum, tamiḻaṟiyātāriṭam tēvippaṭṭāl akkaruttukkaḷic cāṟṟiṭa ēntākavum. āṅkila moḻi marapu vēṟu; tamiḻmoḻi marapu vēṟu; muṉṉatiliruntu piṉṉataṟku moḻi peyarkkum poḻutu ataṉataṉ marapu vaḻakku, moḻiyiyal taṉmi, nuṇukkaṅkaḷ, (idiom, genius and nuances) ākiyavaṟṟi uṇarntu, poruḷ māṟāmal, iyalpāṉa tamiḻil - collukkuc col eṉṟu illāmal - tiṟampaṭamoḻimāṟṟam ceyvataṟku ittakiya irumoḻi vācakaṅkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭāka amivatik kāṇalām.

3. innūliṟ kaṇṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ cila paṟṟic curukkamāka ippatippuriyil kūṟuvatu poruttamākum:-

iyal. 1 :- tamiḻar tamiḻakattiṉ (ēṉ teṉṉintiyāviṉ, intiyāviṉ) tolkuṭikaḷ; avarkaḷiṉ toṉmi teṉṉāṭṭilum intiyāvilum iṟṟikku 50000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarc celkiṟatu eṉpatu mānta marapaṇu aṟiviyaliṉ tēṟṟamāṉa , uṟutiyāṉa iṉṟiya muṭivu ākum. ñāla mutaṉmoḻikku mika neruṅkiyatākiya tamiḻiṉ toṉmiki.mu. 10000kkum muṉṉarp pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ celkiṟatu. intō airōppiya moḻikkuṭumpam (vēta moḻiyum, camaṟkirutamum ikkuṭumpak kuḻantikaḷ), urāl-altāyik moḻik kuṭumpam (cappāṉiyam, koriyam uṭpaṭa), āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻik kuṭumpam [50000 āṇṭukaḷāka āttirēliyāvil uḷḷavi; kaṭanta 10000 āṇṭukaḷāka ulakiṉ piṟa pakutikaḷuṭaṉ yātoru toṭarpum aṟṟavi; āyiṉum iṉṟum tamiḻōṭu viyattaku oṟṟumiyuṭiyavi.] airōppāvil intō airōppiya moḻikaḷ paravuvataṟku muṉṉar aṅku vaḻaṅkiya pāsku-- ākiya moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷukkellām tāyāka iṉṟiya paṉṉāṭṭu moḻiyiyalaṟiñar uṉṉittuḷḷa nācutirātik /yūrēciyāṭik moḻip peruṅkuṭumpatti (Macro family) otta allatu viñciya toṉmiyuṭiyatu tamiḻ. ammoḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭiyē uḷḷa toṭarpukaḷi vērcollāyvukaḷ, moḻiyamippu, ilakkaṇat taṉmi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil viḷakka vēṇṭumāyiṉ tamiḻukkuttāṉ vantāka vēṇṭum; itu aṉṟē kālṭuvel perumakaṉārum, moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇarum niṟuviyatutāṉ. (hāl, hīrās, ñāṉappirakācar, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, illic cuvitic, kīriṉperku, meriṭrūlaṉ, tālkō pāski, antiraṉāv, cuvelapil, vāklāv pilācak, jāṉ peṅṭcaṉ, leviṭ, hakōlā)

iyal 2: cintu nākarikam toṭakkam: kimu.7000; uyarnili : ki.mu.3000- 1500 annākarika moḻi, eḻuttu vaṭivam ākiyavi tamiḻiyam cārntavi eṉpati iṉṟu paṉṉāṭṭu nallaṟiñar tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka ēṟṟuḷḷaṉar. (mārṣal, vīlar, hīrās, kāntiratāv, parpōlā, makātēvaṉ, mativāṇaṉ, pūrṇacantira jīvā, kuruvikkarampi vēlu). tamiḻiya moḻiyum nākarikamum teṉṉintiyāvil iruntu vaṭakku nōkkip paraviyapoḻutu mutaliltōṉṟiyatu cintu nākarikam.

iyalkaḷ 3, 4: iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa (ki.mu.500 - - ki.pi.300 cārnta) caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala iruntu avi (vēṇṭu meṉṟē) tokukkappaṭāmal aḻiyaviṭappaṭṭirukkalām. (kōvar, nevil, spiyars) ilaṅkiyil kiṭittuḷḷa ki.mu.700-ai oṭṭiya tamiḻip poṟippukaḷiyum (muṉivar teraṉiyākala), āticcanallūr, koṟkit (ki.mu.1000-800 cārnta) tamiḻip poṟippukaḷiyum kaṇakkiṟ koṇṭāl - irā.mativāṇaṉ uṉṉippatupōl -- ki.mu.1500kkup piṉṉar cintu eḻuttukkaḷil iruntu, iṭiyīṭiṉṟit teṉṉāṭṭil tamiḻi eḻutturuvam (lipi) uruvākkap paṭṭirukkalām. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ettakiya ciṟanta cemmoḻik karuvūlaṅkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu paṉṉāṭṭuṟiñar pakarntuḷḷavaṟṟi iyal 4liṉ āṟām pakutiyil kāṇka. (tamiḻiṉ, tamiḻariṉ poṟkālam caṅka kālamē ākiyāl innūlil pāti ki.pi.300 variyuḷḷa kālam paṟṟiyatākavē uḷḷatu poruttamē.)

iyalkaḷ 5-8: ivaṟṟil kaḷappirar kālam mutal piṟkālap pāṇṭiyar kālam muṭiya (ki.pi.300-1350) vuḷḷa kālam cārnta varalāṟucurukkamākat tarappaṭukiṟatu; veṟum
aracar paṭṭiyalkaḷ, āṭcik kālaṅkaḷ, tamiḻ aracarkaḷē oruvariyoruvar tākkip peṟṟa veṟṟikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi viṭak kumukāya, paṇpāṭṭuc ceytikaḷukku mutaṉmi tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻnāṭum (ēṉ, intiyāvum) kaṭanta āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka vīḻccippātiyilēyē irunta taṟkuk kāraṇam, 

ētilār ārat tamarpacippar pētiperuñ
celvam uṟṟak kaṭi.

eṉṉum kuṟaḷ kūṟuvatēyām eṉṟu poruḷpaṭa nuṇmāṇuḻipulamikka sṭāṉli vālparṭ (1991) viḷampuvati, iyal 8iṉ iṟutiyil kāṇka; uṇmit tamiḻar uḷaṅ koḷka; uyka.

iyalkaḷ 9-10 ; piṟkālappāṇṭiyar āṭci muṭintu. āṅkilēyar āṭci toṭaṅkiyatu variyuḷḷa kālamākiya ki.pi. 1350 - 1750 paṟṟiyatu iyal 9; 1750 liruntu iṉṟuvari uḷḷa kālam paṟṟiyatu iyal 10. 1991-liruntu tamiḻ nāṭṭukkum nam intiya nāṭṭukkum (ēṉiya āciya āppirikka nāṭukaḷip pōla) peruṅkēṭāka vantuḷḷa veḷḷikkumpiṉiyar poruḷātārak kālaṉi ātikkam (1947kku muntiya) araciyal kālaṉi ātikkatti viṭak koṭumiyāṉatu; annāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷil mukkāl pakutiyiṉarāṉa ēḻikaḷi muṟṟāka oḻippatu; eṉpati iyal 10 viḷakkukiṟatu. tamiḻ maṟṟum ēṉiya intiya moḻikaḷiṉ pātukāppum inta putukkālaṉi ātikkatti liruntu namnāṭu uyvatiltāṉ uḷḷatu eṉpatum teḷivākkappaṭukiṟatu.

4. innūlip paṭikkumpoḻutu, avvappoḻutu iyal 1, iraṇṭām pakutiyil varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa ? eṉṟa pakutiyil varum pēraṟiñar kūṟṟukaḷip pārttāl pala varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷ cārnta, ēṉ?, eppaṭi?, yārukkāka? eṉṟa viṉākkaḷukkāṉa viṭikaḷip paṭippavarkaḷē uṇaramuṭiyum.

5. nūl iṟutiyil 20 pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷa paṭṭiyalil ki.mu 15000liruntu iṉṟuvari tamiḻar varalāṟṟil naṭanta palvēṟu mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷiṉcamakālattil intiyāvilum, ulakiṉ piṟapakutikaḷilum naṭanta mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷ; maṟṟum kali, aṟiviyal cār nikaḻccikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippaṭṭiyalkaḷiliruntu oru paranta, muḻumiyāṉa varalāṟṟu kaṇṇōṭṭam kiṭṭum.

6. innūli uruvākkit tanta pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ āṅkilam - tamiḻ irumoḻikaḷilum vallavar. varalāṟṟāyvilum, moḻiyāyvilum āḻaṅkāl paṭṭavar. moḻinūl katiravaṉ tēvanēyariṉ vaḻinili āyvāḷar. tamiḻppulaṅkaḷil tam paṅkaḷippi iṭiviṭātu ceytu varupavar. āḻnta aṟivum, eḷimiyum, paṇpum uṭiya ivaruṭiya nūlkaḷi kaṭanta kālaṅkaḷil tamiḻ kūṟum ulakukku vaḻaṅkiyatip pōla innūliyum veḷiyiṭṭu eṅkaḷip perumippaṭuttik koḷkiṟōm. itu oru karuvi nūl; oru āyvu nūl. ciṟappumikka ivvaralāṟṟu nūli vāṅki em tamiḻp paṇikku ūkkam tara vēṇṭukiṟōm.

tamiḻar varalāṟu

iṉṟiya nōkkil
(paṇṭu mutal iṉṟu vari)





iyal 1

tōṟṟuvāy

ka. tamiḻaka varalāṟṟiṉ taṉit taṉmi

tamiḻarkaḷ ulakil iṉṟuḷḷa maṟṟa enta moḻi, paṇpāṭṭiyum viṭa atikat toṉmiyāṉatum iṭiyīṭu illātatum āṉa moḻi, paṇpāṭṭiṉ piṟaṅkaṭikaḷ āvar. mānta iṉa nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉ mikac ciṟanta iyalkaḷil oṉṟu tamiḻar marapuc celvam ākum. iṉṟuḷḷa moḻikaḷil māntaṉ toṉmoḻikku mika neruṅkiyatu tamiḻ maṭṭumē. “mutal tāymoḻi” āyvil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷukku oḷi taravallatum tamiḻē. oru ciṟu nilapparappiṉ varalāṟu, paṇpāṭu ākiyavaṟṟic cariyākap purintu koḷḷa ippaṭi mānta iṉattiṉ oṭṭumotta iṉa, moḻi varalāṟukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu paṭuttip pārkka vēṇṭiya niliyil uḷḷa (tamiḻakam pōṉṟa) pakutikaḷ ulakil mikac cilavē; tamiḻ taṉit taṉmi vāyntatu. es.ē.ṭilar kūṟiyuḷḷatu pōl intiya nākarikattiṉ aṭittaḷam tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi – paṇpāṭu ākiyaviyē (S.A. Tyler. “India, an Anthropological perspective (1973) “All of Indian civilization is built on an underlying base of Dravidian language and culture”) “intiya varalāṟṟu āyvu kaḷukkum tamiḻ cārnta āyvukaḷ mika mukkiyamāṉavi. 

2. tamiḻ eṉṉum colliṉ tiripē tirāviṭam (tamiḻ, tamiḷa, tramiḷa, trāmiṭa, trāviṭa). tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa aḻippatu tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpattiyē. tol tirāviṭam (Proto-Dravidian)eṉṟu vaṇṇaṉi moḻi nūlāyvāḷar mīṭṭuruvākkam ceyvaṉavaṟṟuḷ pala piḻiyāy muṭikiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum, paḻantamiḻē itaṟkuk kaṭṭaḷik kallākak koḷḷappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatum, pāvāṇar koḷki. tamiḻiṉ tiripukaḷē piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ. paḻan tamiḻiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭa “tol tirāviṭam” eṉpatu kaṟpaṉiyē eṉpar pāvāṇar. ākavē innūluḷ tirāviṭam, tol tirāviṭam eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭuvaṉavellām uṇmiyil paḻantamiḻiyē kuṟippatākak koḷka.

3. moḻiyiyalil tamiḻiya (Tamulian) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟē kālṭuvel kālam vari vaḻaṅkiya peyari avar tāṉ “tirāviṭa” moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccit tiripu vaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatākum. makāvamcattil “tamiḷa”, taṇṭiṉ eḻutiya avanti cuntarikatiyil“tramiḷa’ eṉṟa collum varukiṉṟaṉa. ikṣvāku parampariyiṉariṉ pirākirutak kalveṭṭilum ‘tamiḷa’ tāṉ varukiṟatu. piṉṉar kumārilapaṭṭariṉ tantira vārttikattil

“tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva” (appaṭi tirāviṭa
(=tamiḻ) maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷil)

eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ-tamiḷa-tramiḷa-tramiṭa-trāviṭa eṉṟu māṟiviṭṭatu. ē.ci.parṉel 1872 intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil mēṟkaṇṭa tantira vārttikap pakutiyi acciṭṭapoḻutu apattamāka

		“āntra trāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva”

eṉṟu acciṭṭār (mēlum paṟpala piḻikaḷuṭaṉ). intat tavaṟāṉa vācakatti kālṭuvel (1875) sṭeṉ kōṉō [Linguistic Survey of India:1906] pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar. ittavaṟi pi.ṭi. cīṉivācaiyaṅkār intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri 42m tokutiyil (1913) teḷḷat teḷivāka niṟuviyuḷḷār. kuññajuṇṇirājā Annals of Oriental Researchtokuti 28 (1979) kaṭṭuriyil kuṟittuḷḷatu pōla parṉel uṭiya tavaṟāṉa vācakattit tamiḻaṟiñar palarum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟi “tamiḻiyum, teluṅkiyum orucērak kumārilapaṭṭar kuṟittār” eṉṟu tavaṟāka eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar!

4. tamiḻ, tamiḻar varalāṟṟic cevvaṉē aṟiya māntar iṉat tōṟṟamum paravalum; moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum paṟṟi iṉṟiya paṉṉāṭṭup paltuṟi aṟiñar ēṟṟuḷḷa ceytikaḷit terintu koḷvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu. (mutalil “varalāṟu” paṟṟi aṟiñar palar karuttukkaḷit tanta piṉṉar acceytikaḷik kāṇalām.)

u. varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa?

		varalāṟu paṟṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ ciṟanta karuttukaḷip potuvāka aṉivarumē terintiruppatu naṉṟu. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉa cilavaṟṟik kāṇka.

“muṟkālattil eṉṉa naṭantirukkalām eṉpatip paṟṟi pintiya talimuṟiyic cārnta māntaṉ oruvaṉ taṉatu maṉattil uruvākkik koḷḷum eṇṇamē varalāṟu ākum. eḻutupavaṉuṭiya aṟivunili, maṉanili, avaṉuṭiya camutāyap pārvi, avaṉukkuk kiṭṭum ātāraṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkēṟpavē oru kālattippaṟṟi allatu oru poruḷippaṟṟi oruvaṉ varalāṟu eḻutukiṟāṉ. eṉavē ovvoru kālam, poruḷ paṟṟi palvēṟu varalāṟukaḷ irukkak kūṭiyatē.”

		“varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ paṭṭaṟivu, paṟpala viṣayaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya avaṉuṭiya kaṇṇōṭṭam ākiyavaṟṟāl uruvāṉa avaṉuṭiya maṉamtāṉ avaṉ eppaṭi varalāṟṟi eḻutu kiṟāṉ eṉpati nirṇayikkiṟatu; viruppu veṟuppaṟṟa varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ muyaṟkomputāṉ. eṉavē enta vara lāṟṟu nūlum muḻumiyāṉa appaṭṭamāṉa uṇmiyik kār uḷḷaḷavum kaṭal nīr uḷḷaḷavum nirṇayittu viṭṭatāka eṇṇiviṭak kūṭātu.”
- kāri pekmāṉ.

“varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ allatu avaṉip payaṉpaṭuttu pavarkaḷ uḷḷattil veḷippaṭiyākavō (allatu avar kaḷukkē teriyāmal āḻmaṉattilō) uḷḷa kuṟikkōḷ kaḷuṭaṉtāṉ varalāṟu eḻutappaṭukiṟatu. akkuṟikkōḷkaḷ piṟa iṉaṅkaḷi, kuḻukkaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttutalum vacappaṭuttutalum; camutāyattiṟku ilakkukaḷik kāṭṭi ūkku vit ta l ; kuḻuk kaḷ, va r k kaṅ kaḷuk ku uṇar c ci yūṭṭu tal , atikāratti ēṟkeṉavē kiyiṟ koṇṭuḷḷavarkaḷukku valuvūṭṭutal; atikāramillātavarkaḷiṭiyē ippoḻutuḷḷa nilimiyē cari eṉṉum pontiki maṉaniliyi ēṟpaṭuttutal pōṉṟaṉavām.”
- jē.ec. piḷamp

		mānta iṉam ceyta koṭumikaḷ, ataṉ mūṭattaṉaṅkaḷ, ataṟku ēṟpaṭṭa tīyūḻ nikaḻccikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ pativē varalāṟu.
- car eṭvarṭ kippaṉ

		(eppoḻutum tīmiyē ōṅkiyiruppatāṉa) inta ulakil naṭappavi niyāyamāṉavi eṉa enta mūṭaṉum nampamāṭṭāṉ.
- ṭākṭar jāṉcaṉ

		“varalāṟṟil perumpakuti uṉṉippu vēli; mīti viruppu veṟuppiṉpaṭiyāṉa kūṟṟu”
- vil & ēriyal ṭurāṉṭ

		“āḷupavarkaḷ, aracukaḷ cārpāka eḻutappaṭum varalāṟukaḷi nampuvatu kolikāraṉ colvati appaṭiyē nampuvatu pōla.”
- cimaṉ vil

		“cevivaḻic ceyti poyyāka irukkum aḷavukku, eḻutiyaviyum kalveṭṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟil cetukkiyaviyum puḷukāka irukkalām”

“pācil paṭivaṅkaḷ uyiriṉa varalāṟukaḷit terivik kiṉṟaṉa. atupōl oru iṉattiṉ moḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷum avviṉattiṉ varalāṟṟi (kuṟippāka ammoḻi pēcunar paṇṭik kālattil ententap pakutikaḷil avvappoḻutu vacittu vantaṉar eṉpati) terivikkavallatu.
- jāṉ pirēcar 1879

“varuṅkālattil eṉṉa naṭakkum eṉak kūṟat tēviyāṉa aṟivi viṭa, paṇṭu eṉṉa naṭantirukkum eṉa uṉṉikkat tēviyāṉa aṟivu mika nuṭpamāṉatu”

aṉatōl pirāṉcu.

“teruvil kampattiṉ ucciyil viḷakku irukkiṟatu - aṉivarum oḷi peṟuvataṟkāka. āyiṉum kuṭikāraṉ ceyvatu eṉṉa? kampattip piṭittuk koṇṭu, tāṉ tariyil viḻuntu puraḷāmal pārttuk koḷkiṟāṉ. varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttuvatu kuṭikāraṉ ceyal pōl irukkak kūṭātu.”

“varalāṟu eḻutuvataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ mikak kuṟivāka irukkum poḻutu nām maṉatiṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu eṉṉa? varalāṟṟu nikaḻcciyi viḷakkappōvatu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa caṭṭaviti oṉṟiṉ kīḻt tīrppu vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatu alla. varalāṟṟu nikaḻcci etuvāyiṉum appaṭṭamāka itutāṉ naṭantatu eṉṟu niṟuviṭa yārālum iyalātu. eṉavē kiṭitta cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ, niyāyamāṉa uṉṉippukkum iṭamaḷittut tāṉ varalāṟṟu nikaḻcciyip paṟṟi eḻuta iyalum.
-W.W. hiṭu

		“varalāṟṟu nikaḻvu oṉṟu kuṟittu iṉṟuvari neṭunāḷāka oru potuvāṉa muṭivu kōlōcci varalām. eṉiṉum ammuṭivil uḷḷa tavaṟukaḷi niṟuva ātāram irukkumāṉāl avvātāraṅkaḷi valiyuṟutta añcak kūṭātu, paḻiya muṭivi māṟṟi (pontiki tarakkūṭiya) putumuṭivi uruvākka uṭaṉaṭiyāka iyalātu eṉṟāl kūṭa. 

		“uṇmiyic coṉṉāl pakiyum kēṭum varalām eṉṟālum uṇmiyi maṟikkātiruppatē nallatu”
-ceyiṉṭ jerōm
		
		“coṉṉāl virōtamatu ākiṉum colluvaṉ”
-nammāḻvār

“camutāyattil nikaḻntuḷḷa māṟṟaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya uṇmikaḷi veḷikkoṇarntu iṉṟuḷḷa karutu kōḷkaḷil putiya oḷi kāṭṭa viḻiyum varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ taṉ ceyalmuṟiyi māṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉkiṟēṉ nāṉ. ērāḷamāṉa
taravukaḷiṭiyē neṭunāḷ uḻaṉṟu koṇṭiruntu piṉṉar avaṟṟiliruntu oru karutukōḷi uruvākka muyalvati viṭa oru eṇṇattukku ātāramuḷḷatu eṉa taṉatu nuṇṇaṟi vukkut tōṉṟiṉāl avveṇṇatti (tāṉ eṇṇiya vāṟō allatu āyvil uruvākkum cemmippāṭu kaḷuṭaṉō) niṟuviṭat taravukaḷ uḷḷaṉavā eṉṟu tēṭuvatē payaṉtarum naṭimuṟiyākum.
-pernāṭ es kāṉ

		(“A moment of insight is worth a month of dead pull”)

oru kāla kaṭṭattil nikaḻnta varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷi viḷakkavalla aṭippaṭik kōṭpāṭṭi āḻamākac cintittu uruvākkāmal, koṉṉē annikaḻccikaḷi maṭṭum kūṟuvatu ticikāṭṭum karuviyiṉṟik kappaṟpayaṇam mēṟkoḷvatākum. kiṭikkum taravukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil taṉatu eṇṇaṅkaḷi uracippārkkāmal, taṉ viruppampōla kōṭpāṭukaḷi uruvākkuvatō maṇal vīṭu kaṭṭuvatu pōṉṟatēyākum.
- nopuru karāṣimā (2001)

 “iṉṟuḷḷa aracāṅkaṅkaḷ eviyum potumakkaḷ nalaṉ kuṟittavi alla. potu makkaḷ peyaric collik koṇṭu avarkaḷ nalaṉukkāka ceyvatākap puḷukik
koṇṭē taṅkaḷ nalaṉiyum mēmpāṭṭiyum nilip paṭuttik koḷḷa paṇakkārarkaḷ ceyalpaṭuttum catit tiṭṭamē ‘aracāṅkam’ ākum.”
- car tāmas mūr

“ituvari ulakil irunta enta araci eṭuttuk koṇṭālum atil nāṭṭāṇmi vakittavarkaḷ kāṟṟuḷḷa pōtē tūṟṟik koḷpavarkaḷum, mūṭarkaḷumē;
iṉi varuṅkālattilum eṉṟikkum itē nili tāṉ nīṭikkum.”
- jāṉ ṭiriṭaṉ

“māntariṉatti aṭimippaṭuttum palvēṟu ecamāṉar kaḷuṭiya akkiramamāṉa kōṭpāṭākiya ‘ellām tamakkē; piṟar ekkēṭu keṭṭāl eṉṉa’ eṉpatiyē paṇakkārarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.”
-ātam smit

“maṉitaṉ oru kōṇalmāṇal kompu; atik koṇṭu nērāṉatu etiyum ceyya iyalātu.”
-immāṉuvēl kāṉṭ

‘oru kuṭumpatti naṭattum poṟuppit toṭṭil kuḻantikaḷiṭam oppaṭippatu pōṉṟatē, “vayatu vantōr aṉivarukkum vākkurimi” aṭippaṭiyil amiyum jaṉa nāyakam ākum.
- āṭṭō vāṉ pismārk

“(cuyanalattukkāka) taṅkaḷāl iyaṉṟa aḷavukkup (piṟarukkum piṟa uyirkaḷukkum) kēṭu ceyya muyalvatum; taṅkaḷik kāttuk koḷḷac ceytāka vēṇṭiya akkiramaṅkaḷic ceytu muṭippatum mānta iṉattiṉ iyaṟki”
-perṭrāṇṭ racal

“nākarikam eṉṟa col kolikārac col - “cutantiram” “jaṉanāyakam” mutaliya coṟkaḷip pōla. nakaraṅkaḷil niliyāka vāḻum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟiyik kuṟippataṟku maṭṭumē ati payaṉpaṭuttukiṟēṉē yoḻiya atu uyarntatu, ciṟantatu eṉṟa poruḷil alla; uṇmiyil accollukku nāṉ cuṭṭum poruḷ maṭṭumē cari. atu ‘uyarnta / ciṟanta’ vāḻkki neṟi eṉṟu potuvākap palarum eṇṇuvatu muḻumūṭattaṉam. “nākarika” makkaḷ ceyaṟpāṭu kaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ cila: paṇṭik kālattil rōm pēraracu āṇṭu tōṟum pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa aṭimi kaḷiyum piṟanāṭṭuk kitikaḷiyum carkkas araṅkil vilaṅkukaḷōṭu caṇṭiyiṭṭuc cākaṭittatu; oruvarukkoruvar koṭūramāka caṇṭiyiṭṭu maṭiyavittatu. iṭikkālattil mekcikō nāṭṭu civappintiya astek maṉṉarkaḷ āṭciyil taṅkaḷ rattaveṟipiṭitta kaṭavuḷ Huitzalopochtli-kku āṇṭutōṟum ēṟattāḻa 50000 aṇṭi nāṭṭukkitikaḷik koṉṟaṉar - uyirōṭu irukkum poḻutē neñcāṅkulikaḷik kattiyāl kutti eṭuttu narapali koṭuttaṉar. speyiṉ nāṭṭil kattōlikka kiṟittava “iṅkuyicicaṉ Inquisition koṭumi taṭimuṟippaṭuttap paṭṭa 1480-1800 il āṇṭutōṟum ammatakkōṭpāṭukaḷi mīṟiyatākak karutappaṭṭa cila ilaṭcam pēr cittiravatik kāḷāyiṉar; uyirōṭu koḷuttappaṭṭaṉar. cerumāṉiya iṭlar 1939-45 kāla aḷavil 60 laṭcam yūtarkaḷi akkiramamākak koṭumippaṭuttik koliceytāṉ, pala vaḻikaḷil - - nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉavarkaḷi aṟiyil aṭittu viṣavāyu celuttik koṉṟatu uṭpaṭa. “nākarika” makkaḷ ceyta immāperum koṭumikaḷi viṭa mōcamāṉa koṭumikaḷi “nākarikamaṭiyāta” makkaḷ evarum ceyta tilli. [koṭumi nikaḻccikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi maṭṭumē mūlam kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. moḻi peyarppil vivaraṅkaḷum viḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
- rōṉālṭu riṭ 1992.

		“cila āṇṭukaḷukkorumuṟi vākkuc cāvaṭikkuc ceṉṟu makkaḷ ōṭṭuppōṭum pōlic caṭaṅkutāṉ jaṉa nāyakam; vaṟumiyil uḻalum perumpāṉmiyiṉar niliyi uyarttakkūṭiya koḷkikaḷi enta nāṭṭu aracum oru poḻutum ceyalpaṭuttātu.” 

-nōvām cāmaski 1994



“muṉṉēṟṟam (vaḷarcci) eṉṟa mōcaṭi vārtti uṇmiyil etik kuṟikkiṟatu eṉpatu paṟṟi nākarika ulakam uṭaṉaṭiyākac cintikka vēṇṭiya āpattāṉa kaṭṭam itu. pireñcu ñāṉi ṭakvil avvārttiyi veṟuttavar. veṟukkātavaṉ iṭlar!”
-jāṉ lūkāks 1999

muntiya varalāṟu iṉṟiya nilimikkāṉa piṉ pulatti viḷakki oḷi tarukiṟatu. uṉatu nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟip paṟṟik koñcamāvatu teriyāmal iṉṟiya nilimiyip puriyavē muṭiyātu. piṟanāṭukaḷip paṟṟiyum avvāṟē. tamiḻaṉ [mūlattil ṭirevalyāṉ ‘āṅkilēyaṉ’ eṉpār] eṉṟa niliyil uṉ iṉattiṉ varalāṟṟit terintu koḷḷāviṭṭāl pala viṣayaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya eṇṇa vōṭṭaṅkaḷ, ātāramaṟṟa cila niṉippukaḷ (prejudices); nikaḻkāla nikaḻccikaḷi nīnōkkumpāṅku (makiḻcci, tuyaram, veṟuppu pōṉṟavi) ākiyavaṟṟik kūṭa nī viḷaṅkik koḷḷa iyalātu.
-ji.em. ṭirevelyāṉ

(paraparappāṉa) taṟkāla varalāṟu nāyiṉ vāl pōṉṟatu. vāl nāyiṉ uṭampil iṟutiyil iruppiṉum mikap paraparappāka āṭum uṟuppu atutāṉ. āyiṉum nāyiṉ uṭampil mukkiyamāṉa uṟuppukaḷ ellām anta vāliṟku muṉṉar uḷḷaviyē!

-ī.hec. ṭāṉs

“oru nāṭṭu makkaḷ enta aḷavukkut tamatu moḻiyip paṟṟi aṟintuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu nī col. anta nāṭu taṉatu taṉittaṉmiyi paṟṟi enta aḷavukkuk kavalippaṭukiṟatu? atu taṉṉik kāppāṟṟik koḷḷumā? eṉpati nāṉ uṭaṉē colli viṭuvēṉ.”
-jāṉ ciyārṭi 1978

“‘vaḷarcci’ eṉpatu (ellāvaṟṟiyum) māṟṟi koṇṭē iruppatu alla; nallaṉavaṟṟik kiviṭāmal iruppatē vaḷarcci... varalāṟṟi maṟappavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ paṭṭa iṉṉalkaḷi iṭiviṭātu mīṇṭum mīṇṭum peṟuvar
-jārj cantayāṉā

“iṉṟu nikaḻvaṉavum, aṉṟu nikaḻntaṉavum iraṇṭumē varuṅkālatti niruṇayikkalām; varuṅkālam eṉpatē iṟanta pi. irāmanātaṉ 9 kālattiliruntu varuvatutāṉ”...... “uṇmi cuṭum, uṇmiyi nām tāṅkik koḷḷa iyalātu.”
-ṭi.es. eliyaṭ

“meyyāṉa nāṇayattuṭaṉ pōli nāṇayamum puḻaṅkum poḻutu piṉṉatutāṉ virintu kimāṟip perumpuḻakkattil irukkum; atēpōl paṇpaṭṭa oḻukkattip pōlik kalācāram viraṭṭiviṭum”
-villiyam kōlṭiṅ

‘varalāṟu eḻutum nām namkālattavar cārpil mānta iṉattil muntiya naṭavaṭikkikaḷip paṟṟi matippīṭu ceykiṟōm. varalāṟṟāyvu ceyvatākak kūṟikkoṇṭu poḻutu pōkkupavar munti varalāṟṟu naṭavaṭikkikaḷik kuṟittu itu aṟivuṭiyatu, atu mūṭattaṉamāṉatu; itu matukiyuṭiyatu, atu kōḻittaṉam; icceyal naṉṟu, acceyal tītu; eṉṟavāṟu matippiṭṭuk kūṟum poṟuppit taṭṭik kaḻittal ollātu. kāyaṭitta varalāṟṟāciriyaṉ namakkut tēviyilli.”
-ār.ji. kāliṅvuṭ

2. varalāṟṟu variviyal (Historiography)māṇavar nalaṉ karuti mēṟkaṇṭa kūṟṟukaḷiṉ āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷ kīḻē tarappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.

		History is a reconstruction of elements of the past in the mind of a human being of a later generation...... In principle there will be multiple histories of any given period, each congruent to the mental world, social purposes, and sources avilable to the person who creates it.

		Since the recreation of the past takes place in the mind of the individual historian which has been shaped by his personal experience and world view, the unbiased historian is an unattinable ideal.

		By the very nature of the historical discourse there can be no final truth - Gary Beckman “The limits of credulity” Journal of American Oriental Society 125.3 (2005)

		(“History”) is always a created ideology with a purpose, designed to control individuals or motivate societies, or inspire classes...... to strengthen the purpose of those who possessed power..... and reconcile those who lacked it.

- J.H. Plumb (1969) The death of the past (quoted by Beckman)

		“History is indeed little more than the register of the crimes follies and misfortunes of mankind” 
- Sir Edward Gibbon

		“Most history is guessing and the rest is prejudice.
- Will and Ariel Durant

		Official history is believing the murderers at their word.
- Simone weil

		“The pen is at least as great a liar as the tongue” 
	“Words may also be fossil history, the history of a nation’s wanderings” 
-John Fraser 1879. Etruscans; were they celts?” Edinburgh.
 
		It needs a rarer genius to restore the past than to foretell the future. 
-Anatole France

		“(Statistics should not be used) as a drunken man uses lamp posts, for support rather than information” 

		“Where the evidence is meagre, it is well to remember that what is at issue is not a point of exact law but an event of history, a field in which absolute truth is unattinable and interpretation must ordinarily resort to a certin amunt of surmise” (W.W. Hyde: Ancient Greek Mariners, N.Y. 1974)

		“It is appropriate to challenge conventional wisdom even where there may be no equally comprehensive wisdom to substitute in its place”

		“If an offence come out of the truth, better is it that the offence come than that the truth be concealed” (St Jerome)

		If the historian is to make breakthrough to new views of social change and to contribute to the development of social theory, I have argued that his mode of work must change. He has to start not with data but with ideas and then ask what data are avilable or could be found to test and refine his ideas.”
- Bernard S. Cohn. Oct 1970. “Society and social change under
the Raj” South Asian Review 4-1.

 		“Studying history devoid of applicable theory is to set to sea without a compass, while postulating theory, free of factual examination is to build a castle on quicksand.
- Noboru Karashima, in his preface to Karashima Omnibus
2001.

		“The modern state is not a commonwealth... (it is) nothing but a certin conspiracy of rich men procuring their own comforts under the name and title of the commonwealth” 
- Sir Thomas More

		

No government has ever been, or can ever be, wherein time - servers and block heads will not be uppermost. 
- John Dryden

		(rich men follow) “the vile maxim of the masters of mankind - all for themselves and nothing for other people” 
-Adam Smith
		“Universal suffrage is the government of a house by its nursery
-Otto Von Bismark

		“Men do as much evil as they can and as much as they must”
-Bertrand Russell

		“Civilization” like “freedom” and “democracy” is a word that kills. I use it in its literal sense, as shorthand for a settled life in towns and cities. If the word means anything at all, it means only this: The moral values commonly attached to it are nonsense. Civilized people have practised the Roman circus, the Aztec sarifice, the Spanish Inquisition, the gassing of jews. Uncivilized ones have behaved no worse.
- Ronald Wright 1992. Stolen Continents - the Indian story.
John Murray; pp 424. see p 98

		Democracy is nothing more than formal voting, while Government policies do not attend to the necessities of the most impoverished”
-Noam Chomsky.. 1994. World Order Old and New

		“the grave task before civilisation now is a necessary rethinking of the entire meaning of progress (a term that Tocqueville abhorred, but Hitler did not)
- John Lukacs. 1999



		History throws light on the present. you cannot understand your own country, still less any other, unless you know something of its history. You cannot even understand your own personal opinions. prejudices and emotional reactions unless you know what is your heritage as an Englishman” 
-G.M. Trevelyan

		Contemporary history is like the til of a dog; it looks more conspicuousley agitated than the rest, but all the most important parts have preceded it” 
-E.H. Dance

		“Tell me how much a nation knows about its own language and I will tell you how much that nation cares about its own identity” 
John Ciardi (Chicago Tribune: 27.8.78). 

		Progress, far from consisting of change depends on retentiveness.... Those who disregard the past are bound to repeat it.
- George Santayana.

		“Time present and time past are both perhaps present in time future”
		“We cannot stand much reality” 
- T.S. Eliott

		“Just as bad money drives out good, so inferior culture drives out superior” 
- William Golding.

		“We are the present of man, passing judgement on his own corporate past. What we cannot do, is to continue playing with historical research and yet shirk the responsibility of judging the actions we narrate; saying this is wise, that foolish; this courageous, that cowardly; this well done, that ill”

		- R.G. Colingwood (1999 posthumous: Ed by W.H. Dray and another) The Principles of history and other writings in philosophy of History. Oxford; OUP.

ṅa. mānta iṉattōṟṟamum paravalum

1. puṭaviyiṉ (pirapañcattiṉ) akavi cumār 1400 kōṭi āṇṭuvari irukkalām. ulakam uṭpaṭa ñāyiṟṟuk kuṭum pattiṉ vayatu ēṟattāḻa 450 kōṭi āṇṭu ākum. ulakil mutaṉ mutalil uyirkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu ēṟattāḻa 300 kōṭi āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉartāṉ. mutalil tōṉṟiyavi ‘pāk ṭīriyā’ pōṉṟa ōraṇu uyirikaḷē. iṉṟuḷḷa aṉittuc ceṭikoṭikaḷum vilaṅku - paṟavikaḷum orē mūlattil tōṉṟiyaviyē ākum. ivvuyiriṉa vakikaḷil 15 ilaṭcam taṉi iṉaṅkaḷ (Species) kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭup peyar cūṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum iṉam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭātaviyum, peyar cūṭṭappaṭātaviyum (kuṟippāka nilittiṇi, nuṇ ṇuyirkaḷ, ciṟṟuyirkaḷ) mummaṭaṅku irukkum eṉṟu aṟiviya lārkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟikku 24 kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar oru taṭaviyum/, āṟarik kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar maṟṟoru taṭaviyum pēraḻivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu antantak kālakkaṭṭattil irunta uyiriṉaṅkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavi aḻintuviṭṭaṉa. (mikap periya viṇ koḷḷikaḷ (Meteors) ulakil viḻuntatāl appēraḻivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉkiṉṟaṉar). ippoḻutu uḷḷa pālūṭṭikaḷ (Mammals) aṉittumē 16 kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar uruvāṉa oru ciṟiya (eli pōṉṟa parumaṉuṭiya) vilaṅkiliruntu paṭimalarcci aṭintaviyē. kuraṅkukaḷukkum māntaṉukkum mūtāti yāṉa ilemūr (Lemur) vilaṅku (ēṟattāḻa 250 kirām eṭi) uruvāṉa kālam āṟarik kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar ākum. (pūccikaḷ tōṉṟi 50 kōṭi āṇṭum eṟumpukaḷ tēṉīkkaḷ tōṉṟi 10 kōṭi āṇṭum, kariyāṉ tōṉṟi 28 kōṭi āṇṭum ākiṉṟaṉa) ippakkattiṟkum muṉ pakkattukkum iṭiyil oṭṭiyuḷḷa “puṭaviyiṉ tōṟṟattiliruntu ikkāla māntaṉ vari” eṉṟa vivarap paṭatti kāṇka.

2. māntaṉ eppaṭit tōṉṟiṉāṉ? māntak kuraṅkiṉat tukkum (cimpaṉci, korillā) māntaṉukkum potuvāṉa vēṟu ōr uyiriṉam iṉṟikku ēṟattāḻa 50 ilakkam āṇ ṭukku muṉ āppirikkāvil iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiviyal muṭivu. iṉṟu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa 650 kōṭi māntarkaḷumē (atāvatu tirāviṭar, intō airōppiyar, maṅkōliyar, cemittiyar, amerikka intiyar ākiya aṉivarumē) hōmō cēpiyaṉs cēpiyaṉs allatu taṟkāla māntar eṉṟa (Home Sapiens Sapiens or Anatomically Modern Humans) eṉṉum orē iṉattic cārntavarkaḷ. ivviṉam āppirikkāvil iṉṟikku oṉṟari ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar vāḻnta orē tāyiṭam iruntu tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟiya aṟiviyalār aṉivarum ēṟṟa muṭivu (Cavalli Sforza and others: The history and geography of Human genes, 1994). ēṟattāḻa māntaṉiyotta “muṉmānta (Hominid)” iṉaṅkaḷ kaṭanta 48 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷil tōṉṟic cila pala ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷ vāḻntu piṉṉar muṟṟilum aḻintoḻintu viṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ hōmō erakṭas eṉṟa iṉamum aṭaṅkum. atu maṭṭumē āppirik kāvi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟip piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷilum paraviyiruntatu. kurōmakṉāṉ maṉitaṉ, pīkiṅ māntaṉ, cāvaka māntaṉ, atti rampākkam pācil māntaṉ ākiyavarkaḷ (ēṟattāḻa 3 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar vāḻntavarkaḷ) inta hōmō erakṭs vakiyic cārntavarkaḷē. airōppāvil 40,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vari vāḻntu piṉṉar aṭiyōṭu aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭa niyāṉṭartal (Neandarthal) iṉamum muṉmānta iṉamē. inta “muṉmānta iṉaṅkaḷ” evaṟ ṟiṭiyēyum “moḻi” uruvākavē illi (None of them had the faculty of speech)

		3. ippoḻutuḷḷa māntarkaḷākiya  AMH vakiyic cārnta nam mānta iṉam, kaṭanta oṉṟari ilaṭcam āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉar āppirikkāvil tōṉṟi ulakeṅkum piṉ varumāṟu paraviyatu - atāvatu,

cipīriyāvukku 		iṉṟikku 	30,000 		āṇṭukaḷukku
										muṉṉarum
airōppāvukku			 ” 	40,000 			”
vaṭa/teṉ
amerikkāvukku 			” 	30,000 - 12,000		”

pacipik tīvukaḷukku 			” 	4000 - 1000 			”
(mikrōṉiciyā, pāliṉīciyā)

paraviṉar eṉpatu vallunar karuttu ākum. iti viḷakkum nilappaṭaṅkaḷ aṭutta iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. kaṇṭaṅkaḷ nakarvuk koḷkiyiṉ paṭi  (Continental drift) kaṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṭanta 25 kōṭi āṇṭukaḷākap pirintu nakarntuḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum ulakil iṉṟu uḷḷa kaṇṭaṅkaḷ ellām ēṟattāḻa ippōtuḷḷa uruvi ēṟattāḻa oru kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē aṭintuviṭṭaṉa. ataṟkup piṉṉar, kaṇṭam aḷavukku (Continental proportions) peru nilappakuti etuvum kaṭalukkuḷ mūḻkavilli. āyiṉum paṉiyūḻi muṭivil ki.mu. 8000 vākkil (paṉikkaṭṭi uruki kaṭal maṭṭam cumār 300 aṭi uyarntatāl ulakeṅkum kaṭalōrap pakutiyākiya kaṇṭattiṭṭu (Continental shelf) ēṟattāḻa - irunūṟu kal aḷavukku kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyatu. avvāṟu akkālak kaṭṭattil tamiḻakattic cuṟṟiyum (teṉ tici uṭpaṭa) cila nūṟu kal (mil) tolivum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyirukkalām. avvāṟu mūḻkiya nilappakutiyiyē kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kaḷaviyal uriyum cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē iṉṟiya aṟiviyalukkup poruntuva tākum. āppirikkāviliruntu taṟkāla māntaṉ ēṟattāḻa 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar āttirēliyā, mēṟku āciyā pōṉṟa pakutikaḷukku pulam  yarntu ceṉṟatu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyāka irukkalām. jiyākirāpikal mākacīṉ cepṭempar 2006 itaḻil taṟkālamāntar āppirikkāvi viṭṭut teṉṉintiyāviyoṭṭiya kaṇṭattiṭṭu vaḻiyākap pulampeyarntu ceṉṟa pātiyik kāṭṭum nilappaṭam uḷḷatu. akkālak kaṭṭattil ippoḻutu uḷḷatu pōlavē ellāk kaṇṭaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa veṉiṉum nilappakuti cila nūṟumil virivāka iruntirukkum. intiyak kari cārnta kaṇṭattiṭṭup pakutiyil atāvatu kariyōrak kaṭaṟpakutiyil (Continental shelf)āḻkaṭal akaḻāyvu ceytāl itu paṟṟiya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkalām eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār piḷemiṅ (2004). iṉṟiya māntaṉiṭam uḷḷavi 23x2 ‘kurōmōcōm’kaḷ. avaṟṟil aṭaṅkiya marapaṇukkaḷ (Genomes) eṇṇikki 30,000. namatu marapaṇukkaḷukkum cimpaṉci marapaṇukkaḷukkum 98.50 viḻukkāṭu oṟṟumi uṇṭu. (oṉṟari viḻukkāṭē marapaṇu niliyil cimpaṉciyiliruntu māntaṉ vēṟupaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟālum māntaṉ evvaḷavu māperum koṭumiyāṉa uyiriyāka māṟi viṭṭāṉ!).



Images from page nos 16 & 17
		4. kaṇṭaṅkaḷ nakarvukkoḷki, maṟṟum kaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷ kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya ceyti ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil kaḻaka nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇum kaṭalkōḷ ceyti kaḷi iṉṟiya aṟiviyalukkēṟpa viḷakka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. kumari muṉikkut teṟkil nilappakutikaḷ kaṭalkōḷil mūḻkiya ceytiyik kalittoki 104m cilappatikāram kāṭu kāṇkātiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyattiṉ mutal urikārar ākiya (ki.pi.10ām nūṟṟāṇṭic cārnta) iḷampūraṇarum itik kuṟippiṭukiṟār. avarukkup piṉ ṉar vanta iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ uriyāciriyarum
aṭiyārkku nallārum icceytiyi mēlum virivupaṭutti caṟṟu mikippaṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam kūṟum ikkaṭalkōḷ ceytiyip paṟṟi eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa āyvurikaḷ varumāṟu:

ca.cōmacuntarapārati (1913) tamiḻp paṇṭi ilakkiyaṅ kaḷum tamiḻakamum, cittānta tīpikā XIV
vi.jē. tampi piḷḷi (1913) : māṇikkavācakar toṉma varalāṟu, tamiḻiyaṉ āṇṭikuvāri II – 1
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (1930) : māṇikkavācakar varalāṟum kālamum
ē.es. vittiyanāta aiyar (1929) : “kīḻināṭukaḷiṉ piraḷayat toṉmaṅkaḷ” : pampāy varalāṟṟuk kaḻaka jarṉal II – 1
jē. periyanāyakam (1941) maṉuviṉ piraḷayam : ti niyū riviyū XI
hīrās pātiriyār (1954) tol intō naṇṇilak kari nākarika āyvukaḷ iyal IV pak. 411-439 catapatapirāmaṇam 1,8 mutaliyavaṟṟil kūṟappaṭum “maṉu piraḷayam” tirāviṭat toṉmattiliruntu uruvākiyatu eṉa (iii) um iv) um kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. cumeriyap piraḷayak katikūṭap paḻantamiḻk kaṭalkōḷ toṉmattiliruntu uruppeṟṟatē eṉa (i) m vi)m karutukiṉṟaṉa.

5. intiyamākkaṭalil paḻaṅkālattil oru kaṇṭam iruntu atu kaṭalil mūḻki viṭṭatu eṉṟu 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu iṟutiyil hēkkalum vēṟu cilarum karuti ataṟku “lemūriyā” eṉṟa peyariyum iṭṭaṉar. āṉāl iṉṟuḷḷa aṟiviya laṟiñarkaḷiṉ orumitta karuttu eṉṉa? kaṇṭam aḷavukkup periya nilap parappu etuvum entak kālattilum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṭa villi eṉpatē avarkaḷ muṭivu eṉak kaṇṭōm. kāntira tāv tamatu “mukkaṭaṟputirkaḷ” nūlil iti 1974lēyē cuṭṭiyuḷḷār. eṉiṉum iti yārum kaṇṭukoḷvatilli! 

“toṉmi nākarikaṅkaḷip paṭitta mānta iṉaṅkaḷik koṇṭirunta (kaṇṭam pōṉṟa) peru nilapparappukaḷ eviyum entak kālattilum intiya, pacipik, aṭlāṇṭik peruṅkaṭalkaḷ evaṟṟilum iruntiruppataṟkāṉa vāyppu aṟavē illi eṉalām”.

		“It is highly improbable that big land masses, inhabited by large numbers of people who created ancient civilizations ever existed in the Indian, Pacific or Atlantic Oceans”. (p267)

ippoḻutu aṟiviyal ēṟṟuḷḷatu kaṇṭa nakarvu maṟṟum pūmippāḷaṅkaḷ kōṭpāṭē (Continental drift and Plate techtonics)yākum. itaṉi viḷakkum ulakap paṭaṅkaḷ iraṇṭi aṭuttapakkaṅkaḷil kāṇka. āyiṉum ki.mu. 8,000ai oṭṭi urm (WURM) paṉiyūḻi iṟutiyil kaṭalmaṭṭam uyarntu ulakeṅkum ēṟattāḻa irunūṟu, munnūṟu kal aḷavukku kaṭaṟkarip pakuti kaṭalil mūḻkiviṭṭatu eṉpati ipputiya kōṭpāṭum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu. akkaṭalkōḷ kālattiṟku muṉṉar intup peruṅkaṭalil uḷḷa tīvukaḷ mūlamāka āppirikkāviyum teṉṉintiyāviyum iṇitta vāl pōṉṟa nilappakutikaḷum cila (piṉṉar mūḻki viṭṭavi) iruntirukkalām eṉavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. (vālṭar pērcarvīs; The Roots of Ancient India, 1971).

6. ilemūriyāk kaṇṭak koḷki nilavi vanta kālattilum kūṭa ca. cōmacuntarapārati, mayili cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi, em.ārōkkiyacāmi pōṉṟa ciṟanta aṟiñarkaḷ kumarikkut teṟkil kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya pakuti kaṇṭam aḷaviṉa taṉṟu; ciṟu nilappakutiyāka irukkalām eṉṟē kuṟippiṭṭuḷ ḷaṉar. kā.appātturiyārum ‘kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭati uṇarka. ippoḻutu ilemūriyāk kaṇṭak koḷki ātāramaṟṟatu eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭu viṭṭatic cākkāka vittuk koṇṭu cumati irāmacāmi taṉatu kaṟpaṉip puviyiyalāḷarkaḷum, pēraḻivu varalāṟu kaḷum; mūḻkiviṭṭa ilemūriyā : Fabulous geographers, catastrophic Histories : The Lost Lemuria (2004 : permaṉeṉṭ piḷāk, ṭelli) eṉṉum nūlil “kaṭalkoṇṭa teṉṉāṭu” paṟṟi eḻutiya tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ aṉivariyum tākki ēḷaṉam ceytuḷḷār. 1950kaḷukkup piṉ “lemūriyāk kaṇṭak” koḷkiyi aṟiviyal aṟavē kiviṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpatu uṇmitāṉ. āṉāl cumati irāmacāmi oṉṟik kuṟippiṭa maṟukkiṟār. atu eṉṉa? kalittoki, cilappatikāram, piṟkāla uriyāciriyar nūlkaḷ cuṭṭum kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu ciṟiya aḷaviṉatāka iruntirukkalām eṉṟu tamiḻaka aracu 1972il veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu – tol paḻaṅkālam pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvati avar kaṇṭu koḷḷavilli. aṉṟiya ‘nāṭu’ eṉpatu ‘intiyā’ ‘tamiḻ
nāṭu’ pōṉṟa perunilap pakutiyaṉṟu. iṉṟiya vaṭṭa (tālukā) aḷaviṟkuḷ kūṭa iraṇṭu - mūṉṟu nāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉavē. uriyāciriyarkaḷ kuṟitta 49 nāṭukaḷum cērntu kaṇṭam aḷavukku iruntirukka vēṇṭiyatēyilli. muṉ pattiyil kūṟiyatu pōla ki.mu. 8,000ai oṭṭi kaṇṭattiṭṭuppakuti kaṭalil mūḻkiyatiyē kaḻaka nūlkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭalkōḷ ceytikaḷ kūṟuvatākak koṇṭāl iṉṟiya aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷukkuk kaḻaka (caṅka) ilakkiyak kaṭalkōḷ ceyti muṟṟilum icivatēyākum eṉpatu tēṟṟam. (kāṇka pi.irāmanātaṉ 1998,2003,2004).

Images from page nos  20 & 21
		7. ivvāṟu tamiḻ, tamiḻar ceytikaḷik koccip paṭutti eḻutuvatu ivvāciriyarukku vaḻakkamāṉatē. ti intiyaṉ ekaṉāmik aṇṭ cōciyal hisṭari riviyū 38-2 ēpral - cūṉ 2001 itaḻil cumati irāmacāmi “iṉaṅkaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷ : tolliyal (tamiḻt) tēciya uṇarvu, cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉpāl īrppu” eṉṟu oru kaṭṭuri eḻutiṉār. cintu veḷi nākarikamum annākarika muttiri eḻuttukaḷum tirāviṭam (toltamiḻ) cārntaviyē eṉṟu ciṟanta āyvāḷarkaḷ kamil cuvelapil (1990: tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal - ōr aṟimukam, iyal 6 : tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum hārappā moḻiyum) askō parpōlā (praṇṭ liṉ ak 24,2000) karutuvatiyellām ak kaṭṭuriyil cumati irāmacāmi kaṇṭu koḷḷavilli. cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ tamiḻac cārpi valiyuṟuttum tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷi yellām (parpōlā, pārcarvīs, cuvalepil pōṉṟavarkaḷ āyvukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil eḻutupavarkaḷ uṭpaṭa), avar eḷḷi nakiyāṭiyuḷḷār.

8. ippakutiyil iṟutiyāka aṟiviyal cārnta iraṇṭu ceytikaḷit taruvatu poruttamākum. avi tamiḻar varalāṟu paṟṟiyaviyalla. eṉiṉum māntaṉ ovvoruvaṉum terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyavi. oru ceyti aṇṭattip paṟṟiyatu. maṟṟatu piṇṭattip paṟṟiyatu.
9. mutalāvatu pirapañcattōṟṟattip paṟṟiyatu. ataṉi āṉanta vikaṭaṉ 10.9.2006 itaḻil aṟiñar cujātā viḷakkiyatu pōl teḷivāka yārālum viḷakka iyalātu. ataṉik kīḻē kāṇka:

“vikaṭaṉ 6.8.06 itaḻil, ‘pirapañcattil aṇukkaḷukku aḻivilli’ eṉṉum pil pricaṉiṉ karuttik kuṟippiṭṭōm.

pricaṉ, pirapañcattiṉ ārampa kaṇatti vivarittu eḻutiyirukkiṟār. Big bang eṉṟu avar collum ārampa veṭit tattuvam ippōtu aṟiviyalāḷarkaḷāl potuvāka oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭu irukkiṟatu.

oru purōṭṭāṉ eṉpatu ettaṉi ciṟiyatu? ataṉ ‘ṭ’ṭaṉṉāviṉ puḷḷiyiṉ parimāṇattil 500,000,000,000 purōṭṭāṉkaḷi aṭakka muṭiyum. allatu aintu laṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku ettaṉi cekaṇṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉavō, ēṟattāḻa attaṉi purōṭṭāṉkaḷi aṭakka muṭiyum.

		inta purōṭṭāṉi 100 kōṭi pākamākkiṉāl ēṟpaṭum avvaḷavu ciṟiya iṭiveḷiyil, oru avuṉs mēṭṭar eṉṉum parupporuḷi aṭittu pāruṅkaḷ... nīṅkaḷ oru pirapañcatti ciruṣṭikkat tayār! parimāṇamaṟṟa attaṉi ciṟiya iṭattil pirapañcattiṉ attaṉi parupporuḷkaḷiṉ cāttiyaṅkaḷum aṭarntirunta anta ārampa kaṇatti ‘orumitta kaṇam’ (ciṅkulāriṭṭi - singularity) eṉkiṟārkaḷ. atiliruntu, kāraṇamiṉṟi tiṭīreṉa attaṉi caktiyum veṭittuc citaṟip paraviyatu.

itit taḷḷi niṉṟu yārum pārttirukka muṭiyātu. kāraṇam, taḷḷi niṟka iṭam etuvum iruntirukkātu. ‘spēs’ eṉṉum viṇveḷiyē antac citaṟaliṉpōtu uṇṭāṉatu tāṉ. antak kaṇattukku muṉ eṉṉa iruntatu eṉṟum colla muṭiyātu. kāraṇamum, kālamum appōtutāṉ tuvaṅkiyatu!

maṉacil eṇṇip pārkkak kaṣṭamāka irukkiṟatallavā? nammāḻvār muyaṟci ceytirukkiṟār. ‘oṉṟum tēvum ulakum uyirum maṟṟum yātumillā aṉṟu...’ pirapañcam appaṭit tāṉ ārampittatu eṉkiṟatu kāsmālaji. ‘oṉṟum illi’yil iruntu ‘ellām uṇṭu’ eṉkiṟa māṟutal nikaḻnta oru mantira upupupa kaṇattiṉ piriviltāṉ īrppu vicikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iyaṟpiyal vitikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
mutal nimiṣattil pirapañcam pala kōṭi nūṟāyiram milkaḷ paraviviṭṭatu. iṉṟuvari virivaṭintukoṇṭē irukkiṟatu. anta pilliyaṉ ṭikiri uṣṇattil, mutalil hiṭrajaṉ, hīliyam pōṉṟa eḷiya taṉimaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.

ārampitta mūṉṟu nimiṭaṅkaḷil maṟṟa attaṉi taṉimaṅkaḷum uṇṭākiviṭṭaṉa. ivi tāṉ pirapañcattil iṉṟum puḻakkattil uḷḷaṉa. etaṟkum aḻivē kiṭiyātu. oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟāka māṟuvatutāṉ, parupporuḷ caktiyāka, cakti parupporuḷāka... itiyellām eppaṭik kaṇṭupiṭit tārkaḷ? pakkattil iruntu pārttatu pōlac col kiṟārkaḷē, eppaṭi? 

anta ārampa kaṇattil iṉṟiya viḷivukaḷ, viṇ veḷiyil eṅku nōkkiṉum oru kāsmik mikrōvēv piṉpula veḷiccamākat terikiṟatu. naṭcattiraṅkaḷ aṉittum vilaki vilakic celvataṟku attāṭciyāka viṇveḷiyil tūrattu naṭcattiraṅkaḷiṉ oḷiyi spekṭrāskōp mūlam pārkkumpōtu, reṭ ṣiḵpṭ eṉṟa niṟa māliyiṉ civappu niṟa iṭamāṟṟam iruppatu terikiṟatu. itiliruntu ārampa kaṇattik kaṇakkiṭṭu irukkiṟārkaḷ. iṉṟikku ēṟattāḻa 13.7 pilliyaṉ (200 milliyaṉ muṉṉē piṉṉē irukkalām) āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nikaḻntatām. (pilliyaṉ eṉpatu 100 kōṭi; milliyaṉ 10 laṭcam)”

10. (i) iṉṟu ulakil uḷḷa 600 kōṭi māntar aṉivarumē ēṟattāḻa oṉṟari ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar āppirikkāvil vāḻnta oru peṟṟōr allatu kuḻuviṉariṭamiruntu tāṉ uruvākiyuḷḷaṉar eṉpati iṉṟu viññāṉikaḷ eppaṭi niṟuviyuḷḷaṉar? ovvoru maṉitaṉiṉ uṭalil uḷḷa ticu (cel)kaḷ eṇṇikki 100 ṭirilliyaṉ (= 1 kōṭi kōṭikaḷ = 1014). ivaṟṟuḷ irattac civappaṇuk kaḷākiya celkaḷ tavirap piṟa ovvoṉṟukkuḷḷum oru miyakkūṟu nucleus uḷḷatu. akkūṟu ovvoṉṟukkuḷḷum DNA  eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum 23 iṇi kurōmocōmkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ 22 iṇikaḷ āṇukkum peṇṇukkum potuvāṉavi. 23vatu iṇi peṇṇukku X, X  eṉṟum āṇukku x, y eṉṟum irukkum. y kurōmōcōm āṇ vaḻiyākavē varum. ovvoru cellukkuḷḷum (ataṟkuḷ irattattiṉ mūlam vantucērum kuḷukōs, uyirvaḷi, pōṉṟavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭutti) vētiyiyal ceyalpāṭukaḷi nikaḻttum - energy metabolism - miṭakāṇṭiriyā (mitochondria) eṉṉum mīcciṟu amippukaḷum pala uḷḷaṉa. miṭakāṉṭiri yāvukku ‘celliṉ DNA’ viliruntu māṟupaṭṭa taṉi DNA uṇṭu. āṇ vintaṇuvum peṇ karu muṭṭiyum cērntu māntak karu uruvākumpoḻutu peṇ karuviliruntu maṭṭumē miṭakāṉṭiriyākkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaru muṭṭiyiviṭa mika nuṇṇiyatāṉa āṇ vintaṇuvil miṭakāṇṭiriyā iruppataṟku iṭamēyilli. eṉavē tāyvaḻiyākavē miṭakāṭiriyākaḷi avaḷ makaḷkaḷum makaṉkaḷum peṟuvar. āṉāl makaḷkaḷiṭamiruntu tāṉ aṭutta talimuṟikku miṭakāṉṭiriyā cella iyalum. talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka miṭakāṇṭiriyākkaḷ tāy - makaḷ eṉṟavāṟu iṟaṅki varumpoḻutu ovvoru talimuṟiyilum miṭakāṉṭiriyā DNA amippu ettaṉi viḻukkāṭu māṟutal aṭiyum eṉpati aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭittuviṭṭatu. āyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku enta aḷavu mi DNA māṟṟamaṭiyum eṉṟu nirṇayikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatāl akkaṇakkiṉpaṭi piṉṉōkkic ceṉṟu viññāṉikaḷ iṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka kaṇṭupiṭittu niṟuviyuḷḷatu eṉṉa? iṉṟu ulakil uḷḷa aṉittu māntarkaḷumē cumār 1,50,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar āppirikkāvil vāḻntuvanta oru iṇiyar allatu oru kuḻuviṉariṉ piṟaṅ kaṭikaḷ eṉpatē atu. “ellōrum ōriṉam”. [cellil uḷḷa DNA vil 23vatu kurōmōcōmil āṇkaḷāyiṉ uḷḷa la kurōmōcōm māṟṟam aṭiyum vikitattik kaṇṭupiṭittu ammāṟṟa vikitattip piṉṉōkkik kaṇakkiṭṭuc ceṉṟa poḻutu kiṭitta muṭivum miṭakāṉṭiriyā DNA aṭippaṭiyil kiṭitta muṭivic cariyeṉṟu meyppittuḷḷatu]

(ii) mēṟkuṟittu āyvukaḷiliruntu viññāṉikaḷ mēlum niṟuviyuḷḷatu: iṉṟikku 70,000-50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarttāṉ āppirikkāvi viṭṭu taṟkāla mānta iṉam pulampeyarntu ulakiṉ piṟa kaṇṭaṅkaḷukkup paraviyatu eṉpatākum. avvāṟu āppirikkāvi viṭṭu peyarntu paraviya māntap piriviṉaril mukkiyamāṉa oru pirivu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyākac (teṉṉintiyā eṉpatu akkāla kaṭṭattil kaṭalōra nilappakutiyāka irunta kaṇṭattiṭṭu Continental shelf  pakutiyum cērntatu) ceṉṟu cilar aṅkēyē nilittiṭa vēṟu cilar āstirēliyā vari kaṭalōra nilappakuti vaḻiyāka metu metuvāka atāvatu oru talimuṟikku oṉṟiraṇṭu mil tolivu vītam) payaṇittu ceṉṟaṉar. (akkaṇṭattil) āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ eccaṅkaḷ ki.mu. 50000 mutal uḷḷaṉa.

 (iii) iṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷuḷ avvāṟu iṉṟikku 70000-50000 āṇṭu muṉṉar āppirikkāvi viṭṭu nīṅki āstirēliyāvaric ceṉṟaṭinta māntariṉ piṟaṅkaṭikaḷ perumaḷavil uḷḷaṉar eṉpati mi DNA āyviṉ mūlam niṟuviyuḷḷavar maturi kāmarācar kalik kaḻaka aṟiviyalaṟiñar ṭākṭar irāmacāmi piccappaṉ āvār. 23.8.2007, 16.4.2008 tētikaḷil avar ceytittāḷkaḷukku nērkāṇalkaḷil terivitta ceytikaḷ varumāṟu 

“āppirikkāvi viṭṭu iṉṟikku 70000-50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar veḷiyēṟiya oru kuḻuviṉariṭam mi DNA  aṭiyāḷak kuṟiyīṭu M130 tōṉṟiyatu. iṉṟuḷḷa āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ tokiyiṉaruḷ iraṇṭil oruvariṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu.

“maturip pakutiyil uḷḷa makkaḷ palar mi DNA  vi āyvu ceytatil avarkaḷil 5rūliruntu 7% naparkaḷiṭam inta M130 uḷḷatu eṉṟu teriya vantatu. aṉṟiya kaṭaṟkarip pakuti vaḻiyāka mānta iṉattiṉ oru pirivu āppirikkāvi liruntu kiḻakku nōkkip pulam peyarntatu eṉpati inta āyvu meyppittatu (Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences; USA; 2001) piriṭṭaṉ nāṭṭu marapaṇuviyalaṟiñar car
vālṭar pāṭmar coṉṉatu pōl. “nām aṉivarum āppirikkāviliruntu pulam peyarntu vantavarkaḷiṉ piṟaṅkaṭikaḷē” (inta M130 ai piccappaṉ mutalil kaṇṭu piṭittatu ucilampaṭṭi arukē jōti māṇikkam kirāmam āṇṭit tēvar makaṉ tiru virumāṇṭi avarkaḷiṭam tāṉ)

“intiya makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum mi DNA aṭiyāḷak kuṟiyīṭukaḷil mikat toṉmi vāyntatu M130 tāṉ. ativiṭap paḻimiyāṉa mi DNA  kuṟiyīṭukaḷ intiyāvil evariṭamum illi. virumāṇṭiyiṭamum vēṟu cilariṭam kaṇṭa inta M130 kuṟiyīṭu niṟuviyuḷḷatu eṉṉa?  āppirikkāvil ikkāla mānta iṉam AMH  uruvākiya piṉṉar akkaṇṭatti viṭṭu mutalil pulam peyarntu intiyāvil mutalil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷiṉ piṟaṅkaṭikaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷaṉar eṉpatē atu.”

ca. moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum

muṉmānta iṉaṅkaḷ aṉittum pēccāṟṟal aṟ ṟavi. avarkaḷiṭiyē moḻi uruvākavilli, ēṟattāḻa 11/2 ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉāl tōṉṟiya taṟkāla mānta iṉattiṭiyē (AMH) maṭṭumē ēṟattāḻa 50000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar moḻi uruvākiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟiya palatuṟi aṟiviyalāḷariṉ orumitta muṭivākum.

(inti, mitili, rājastāṉi mutaliyavaṟṟi orē inti moḻiyākak koḷvatā? taṉittaṉi moḻikaḷākak koḷvatā? itu paṟṟi ellām moḻiyiyalāḷariṭam karuttu vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. 6000 allatu 6059 eṉpatellām oru
kuttumatippēyākum.) inta 6059 moḻikaḷil 4 viḻukkāṭu ākiya 242(8+72+162) moḻikaḷip pēcuvōr eṇṇikki yāṉa 576 kōṭiyāṉatu mottam 600 kōṭi makkaḷuḷ 95 viḻukkāṭu ākum. mīti 96 viḻukkāṭu ākiya 5817 moḻikaḷip pēcuvōr veṟum 24 kōṭi makkaḷēyāvar (aṟunūṟu kōṭiyil avarkaḷ veṟum nāṉku viḻukkāṭṭiṉarēyāvar).

2. iṉṟu ulakil uḷḷa moḻikaḷ aṉittum orē mutaṉ moḻiyiliruntu tōṉṟiyirukka vāyppuṇṭu eṉṟa koḷki 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ māksmullarukkum ici vāṉatē. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ñāla mutaṉ moḻi āyvil īṭupaṭṭa mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ petarcaṉ, tirāmpetti, cuvā tecu, kirīṉperk, meriṭ rūlaṉ, illic cuvitic, tāl kōpālski, cevarōskiṉ, stārōstiṉ, pāmhārṭu maṟṟum kerṉs, jāṉ peṅṭcaṉ, vāklāv pilācak pōṉṟa palarāvar. palvēṟu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiyum piṉvarumāṟu oru cila peruṅkuṭumpaṅkaḷukkuḷ (Super families) aṭakkalām eṉpatu avarkaḷ kaṇṭuḷḷa uṇmiyākum.
i) nāstirāṭik (intō-airōppiyaṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, urālik, altāyk, kārtvelliyaṉ, āprō- ēciyāṭik-atāvatu cemittiya-hāmittiyak kuṭumpam, ākiya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ itil aṭaṅkum). kirīṉperk vakuttuḷḷa yūrēciyāṭik peruṅkuṭumpattukkum itaṟkum atika vēṟupāṭu illi. yūrēciyāṭik-kil aṭaṅkiyavi etrus kaṉ, intō-airōppiyaṉ, urālik-yūkākīr, altāyk, koriyaṉ-cappāṉiyam-aiṉu, kilyāk, cukōtiyaṉ, eskimōalyūt ākiya moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷākum.

(2000 akṭōparil sṭāṉpōrṭu palkalikkaḻakattil patri rājū kiruṣṇamūrttiyi kirīṉperk cantitta poḻutu, “tirāviṭa moḻi yūrēciyāṭik peruṅkuṭumpattiṉ cakōtari yāka irukkalām. makaḷāka irukka muṭiyātu” eṉat terivit tuḷḷār (Bh. Krishnamurthi. The Dravidian Languages; 2003, pak.46). itiliruntu slāv, irāṉiyaṉ, vētamoḻi/ camas kirutam, kirīk, ilattīṉ, keltik, cerumāṉiyam pōṉṟa moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkellām tāyāṉa intō airōp piyatti viṭat toṉmi vāyntatu tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam, atāvatu paḻantamiḻ eṉṟu uṇaralām.)

		ii. cīṉa-kākēciyaṉ
		iii. āstirik (muṇṭā pōṉṟavi)
		iv. amerikka intiya moḻikaḷ
		v. intō-pacipik
		vi. kōycāṉ
		vii. kāṅkō-cakārā

palvēṟu moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷi iṇittu avaṟṟukku mūlamāṉa moḻip peruṅkuṭumpaṅkaḷik kāṇum āyvāḷar kaḷuḷ cilar ataṟkum mēlē pōy ñāla mutaṉmoḻi (māntaṉiṉ mutal tāymoḻi) āyvukkum ceṉṟuḷḷaṉar. meriṭ rūlaṉ taṉatu moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟam-moḻikaḷiṉ koṭivaḻi āyvu (sṭāṉpōrṭu palkalikkaḻakam, 1994) nūliṉ pakkam 277 il kūṟuvatu varumāṟu: “perumpālāṉa moḻiyiyalaṟiñarkaḷ ottukkoḷḷāviṭiṉum allatu aiyappāṭṭuṭaṉ karutiṉum iṉṟuḷḷa moḻikaḷ aṉittum  (iṉṟu iṟantuviṭṭa āṉāl muṉṉar nilaviyataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭṭiyuḷḷa moḻikaḷ uṭpaṭa) orē ñāla mutaṉ moḻiyiliruntu tāṉ tōṉṟiṉa eṉpataṟku valuvāṉa ātāram uḷḷatu” annūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ 277-366il 27 mukkiyamāṉa karuttukaḷukkup palvēṟu moḻikkuṭumpaṅ kaḷilum uḷḷa coṟkaḷ “Global Etymologies” tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. akkaruttukkaḷukku ñāla mutaṉ moḻiyil eṉṉa vērc col iruntirukkalām eṉṟu ūkikka atu muṉṉōṭi yākum. avaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟukku (ēṟattāḻa 17kku)t tamiḻc coṟkaḷē poruḷ potintaṉavākavum ñālamutaṉ moḻiyiṉ vērccol vaṭivi viḷakkuvaṉavākavum amintuḷḷaṉa.

3. irāparṭ kālṭuvel 1856il taṉatu tirāviṭa atāvatu teṉ intiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam eṉṉum māperum nūli veḷiyiṭṭār. (tiruntiya virivāṉa iraṇṭām patippu 1875) oru puṟam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum maṟupuṟam,

		intō - airōppiya moḻikaḷ
		cemittiya moḻikaḷ
		elāmiṭ moḻi
		cittiya (ippoḻutu ‘urāl-alṭāyk’) moḻikaḷ
		cappāṉiya moḻi
		āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi moḻikaḷ

ākiyavaṟṟukkum iṭiyē kāṇum ilakkaṇa oppumikaḷ, col oppumikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟi ērāḷamāṉa ceyti kaḷi avar tantār. immoḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ piriyumuṉṉar irunta (māntaṉ toṉ moḻiyiṉ) niliyi viḷakkuvataṟkāṉa oḷiyi ñāla mutaṉ moḻikku mika neruṅkiya tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpattic cārnta moḻikaḷiliruntutāṉ peṟṟāka vēṇṭum eṉpati acceytikaḷ valuvāka niṟuviṉa. kālṭuvel kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyati mēlum nuṇuki ārāyntu “mānta iṉa mutaṉ moḻi oṉṟākattāṉ iruntirukka vēṇṭum; mikat toṉmi vāynta toltamiḻē (toltirāviṭam eṉa aḻikka virumpuvār avvāṟē aḻikkalām, maṟuppilli) aṉittu moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷukkum (intō-āriya moḻikaḷ uṭpaṭa) mūlamāṉa moḻiyākak karutappaṭa vēṇṭum” eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭi mēlum āḻamāka virivāka niṟuviyavarkaḷ nallūr cuvāmi ñāṉap pirakācarum ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇarum āvar. intō airōppiya moḻikkuṭumpa moḻikaḷiṉ (ēṉ piṟamoḻikkuṭumpa moḻikaḷukkum tāṉ) aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟukku ñāṉappirakācarum pāvāṇarum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa poruttamāṉa tol-tirāviṭa vērc coṟkaḷi iṉam kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. 1953il ñāṉappirakācar terivitta karuttu varumāṟu:-

				“intō airōppiya moḻikaḷil ‘vēr kaḷ’ eṉa uṉṉikkappaṭupavi poruḷ toṭar paṟṟa veṟum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tām. tirāviṭa moḻi vērkaḷōveṉil aiyattiṟkiṭa miṉṟip poruḷi yum kāraṇattiyum kāṭṭupaviyāka uyirōṭṭa muḷḷa muḷikkaru pōlat tōṉṟum; entak karuttōṭṭattil palvēṟu coṟkaḷ uruvāyiṉa eṉpatit teṟṟeṉa avi kāṭṭum vakiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. ittaṉmi avaṟṟukku il lātatāl intō airōppiya “vērkaḷ” iruḷ maṇṭiya poruḷaṟṟa veṟum olik kuviyalkaḷā kavē tōṉṟum. avaṟṟukkum teḷivu tantu oḷi kāṭṭi uyirūṭṭam tarakkūṭiyavi tirāviṭamoḻi vērkaḷtām.”

piṟamoḻi vērkaḷukkum ikkaruttu perum aḷavukkup poruntuvatākum. ceṉṉik kiṟistuvak kallūri itaḻil  (1929) ñāṉappirakācar “moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟam” eṉṟa kaṭṭuri yil “intō airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ tantiyākiya toṉ moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷ tamiḻil tāṉ kiṭṭum” eṉa muḻaṅkiyiruntār. (Tamil supplies this long looked for clue to finding the true origin of the proto Indo – European language) 

4. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum piṟa moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa uṟavukaḷ kuṟittu ituvari piṉvarum āyvukaḷ nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa:

5. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa mika neruṅkiya oppumiyi pi.irāmanātaṉ (kuppam) tirāviṭap palkalik kaḻakattiṉ tirāviṭiyaṉ sṭaṭīs 1-3; ēpral - cūṉ 2003 itaḻil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭuri virivāka niṟuvukiṟatu. tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar teṉintiyāvil kaṇṭippāka 10,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē iruntu irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum iṅkiruntu ataṟku muṉṉarē āstirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tol tamiḻ makkaḷiṭamiruntu pirintu ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum akkaṭṭuri niṟuvukiṟatu.

ru. tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paraviyatu
teṟkiliruntu vaṭakku nōkkiyē yākum;

tamiḻar intiyāviṉ tolkuṭikaḷ

		1. ippoḻutu pala nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa paltuṟi aṟiñar kaḷum potuvāka ēṟṟuk kikkoṇṭuḷḷa (tavaṟāṉa) karutu kōḷiṉpaṭi toltirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar tāyakam vaṭa kiḻakku īrāṉ pakutiyākum; aṅkiruntu cumār ki.mu. 3000ai oṭṭi avarkaḷ intiyā, teṉṉintiyā, ilaṅkiyi nōkki atāvatu teṟku nōkki iṭam peyarntu ceṉṟaṉar; cellum vaḻiyil, pēruntu varumpoḻutu āṅkāṅku cilar iṟakki viṭappaṭuvatu pōla, tirāviṭa moḻipēcum kuḻukkaḷ cila āṅkāṅku viṭṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. (kē.vi. cuvalepil (1972): “tirāviṭarkaḷ iṟakkam” The descent of the Dravidians; tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal paṟṟiya paṉṉāṭṭu āyvitaḻ (IJDL) tokuti 2; pak. 57-63. pirittāṉiyak kalik kaḷañciyattil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ paṟṟi avar eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭṭuriyilum avar ik karuttiyē kūṟukiṟār. tamatu teṉṉintiya varalāṟu (4m patippu 1976) nūlil kē.ē.nīlakaṇṭa cāstiriyum itu “naṭan tirukkak kūṭātatu alla” (not unlikely) eṉkiṟār.

2. itaṟku nērmāṟāṉa koḷki tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar vaṭakku nōkkip paraviṉar eṉṉum “tirāviṭar ēṟṟam” koḷkiyākum: DRAVIDIAN ASCENT (from south). ippoḻutu atu paḻiya pāṇik koḷkiyākak karutap paṭiṉum ataṉi valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷuḷ her. ār. hāl; hīrās pātiriyār, pi.ti.cīnivāca aiyaṅkār, vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar, maṟimali aṭikaḷ, yu.ār.ehareṉ pels, cēviyar.es taṉināyaka aṭikaḷār, ka.ta.tirunāvuk karacu, tēvanēyappāvāṇar, piriṭjeṭ maṟṟum rēmāṇṭ ālciṉ (1988), kē.kē.piḷḷi ākiyōrum aṭaṅkuvar. cuvirā jeysvāl (1974); jē.ār.mār (1975); parṭaṉ sṭeyṉ (1998) ākiyōr ‘tirāviṭar ēṟṟam’ eṉṟa koḷkiyi ēṟkā viṭiṉum “tirāviṭar iṟakkak” eṉṟa koḷkiyiyum ēṟka maṟukkiṉṟaṉar.)

3. tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar ki.mu. 3000ai oṭṭi intiyā vukkuḷ nuḻintaṉar eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu mēlē pirivukaḷ (ṅa), ākiyavaṟṟil coṉṉavaṟṟōṭu porunti varukiṟatā? moḻip peruṅ kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkiṭiyēyuḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muntiya uṟavukaḷip paṟṟi āyvu ceyyum aṟiñar palar ittavaṟāṉa kōṭpāṭṭiṉi oṭpam iṉṟip piṉpaṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ār.piḷeñc em.spiriks 1997il tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭa tolliyalum moḻiyum: (1) kōṭpāṭṭu āyvuneṟik karuttōṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉṉum nūlil reṉpurū piṉvarum muṭivik kūṟukiṟār:-

	“(mattiya kiḻakkup pakutikaḷil iruntu vēḷāṇmit toḻil ceyta makkaḷ paṇṭu paravat toṭaṅkiyatu paṟṟiya) tolliyal cāṉṟu kaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tol intō airōp piyaṉ, tol āprō - ēciyāṭik, tol elāmiṭ, tirāviṭam, tol alṭāyk moḻi kaḷ ākiya moḻikaḷip pēciyōr aṉivarum mikac curuṅkiya miyak kiḻakku nilappakutiyil (cumēriyā, elām, iṉṟiya turukki) uṭaṉ uṟintu irukka vēṇṭum. immoḻikaḷ ellām (nāstirāṭik moḻiyiyalāḷar kūṟuvatu pōla) toṭarpuṭiyaviyāka iruppatu uṇmi yāṉāl avarkaḷellām appakutiyi viṭṭup pirintu vilakiya ki.mu. 8000-6000 kālakkaṭṭattukku muṉṉar orucila āyiram āṇṭukaḷ immoḻikaḷ aṉittiṟkum mūlamāṉa nāstirātik moḻi an nilappakutiyil pēcappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu tolliyal kaṇṭupiṭippukkaḷukkum ottu varum tarukkamākum.”

ivvāṟu tol - nāstirātik pēciyavarkaḷ aṉi varum 10,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar oruṅkē naṭu kiḻakkup pakutiyil vāḻntiruntārkaḷ eṉṟa intak kōṭpāṭṭiyum (ataṉ toṭarpāṉa “intiyāvukkuḷ aṅkiruntu tirāviṭarkaḷ iṟaṅkiyatu ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 3000ai oṭṭittāṉ” eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭiyum) poyppippatu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyē uḷḷa neruṅkiya piṇippu ākum. itupaṟṟi kāliṉpi. mācikā 1999 il kūṟiyatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu.

“teṉ āciyāvil kaḻipaḻaṅkālattil oru potuvāṉa mūtāti moḻi iruntirukka vēṇ ṭum. taṟpōtiya mānta iṉam (ēṟattāḻa ōr ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar) ulakeṅkum paravat toṭaṅkiya kāla kaṭṭamē tol tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālam ākalām” (It may be a question of a very ancient common substratum in South Asia, Pre-Dravidian going back even to the original peopling of the world; The year Book of South Asian Languages and Linguistics, 2001 New Delhi).

		4. ēṟattāḻa oṉṟari ilaṭcam āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ṉart taṟkāla mānta iṉam āppirikkāvil tōṉṟiyatu; mutal tāymoḻi ēṟattāḻa 50000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar uruvāṉatu; iṉṟikku ōr ilaṭcam - 50,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar iruntu teṉṉintiyā vaḻiyākavum ulakiṉ pala pakutikaḷukkum taṟkāla mānta iṉam paraviyatu eṉṟum mēlē kaṇṭōm. ivvāṟu vaṭakku, vaṭakiḻakku nōkkiya mānta iṉap paravalil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarukku mukāmiyāṉa paṅku iruntirukka vēṇṭum. tirāviṭar ēṟṟam (Dravidian ascent) paṟṟiya intak kōṭpāṭṭi “ñāṉappirakācar – tēva nēyaṉ kōṭpāṭu” eṉa aḻikkalām. (tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷukku āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷuṭaṉ uḷḷa neruṅkiya toṭarpiyum (moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷ piṟa vaṟṟuṭaṉ uḷḷa neṅkiya toṭarpiyum) viḷakka vallatu ik kōṭpāṭēyākum. teṉṉintiyāviliruntu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar uruvākkiyatē cintuveḷi nākarika mākum. (hīrās 1953; mativāṇaṉ 1995; irāmanātaṉ 1999; pūraṇa cantirajīvā 2004) avarkaḷukku elām, cumēriyā, ekiptu mutaliya nākarikaṅkaḷi uruvākkiyatilum paṅku iruntirukka vēṇṭum.

5. cuvelapil (1990) “ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉar tirāviṭam, urāl - alṭāyk, cappāṉiyam moḻi pēcunar kaḷiṭiyē neruṅkiya toṭarpu iruntirukka vēṇṭum” eṉṟu karutuvatik kaṇṭōm. attakiya toṭarpiyum “tirāviṭar ēṟṟam” eṉṉum kōṭpāṭē viḷakkavallatu. ñāṉappirakācar - tēvanēyaṉ kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi ki.mu. 10,000kku muṉṉarē tol intō-airōppiyam pēcunar tol-tirāviṭam pēcu nariṭamiruntu pirintu viṭṭaṉar; miya āciya sṭeppi pul veḷikaḷil cila āṇṭukaḷ vāḻntaṉar; avarkaḷil cila kuḻu viṉar ki.mu. 4000-3000 aḷavil mēṟku nōkki airōppāvukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. (avarkaḷiṭam piṉṉar uruvāṉaviyē kirīk, ilattīṉ, kelṭik, jermāṉik, slāviya moḻik kuṭumpaṅ kaḷākum); vēṟu cila kuḻukkaḷ kiḻakku - teṉkiḻakku nōkki iṭam peyarnta kālam ki.mu. 2500ai oṭṭi ākum; avvāṟu kiḻakkē vanta kuḻuviṉar moḻikaḷil atāvatu intō āriya, irāṉiya moḻikaḷil paṇṭu (atāvatu ki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉar tol tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉariṭamiruntu pirinta kālat til) uṭaṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟa tol tirāviṭa moḻik kūṟu kaḷōṭu cērttu, vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilum appālum vāḻntu vanta tirāviṭa moḻipēcunarkaḷiṭam iruntu (iraṇ ṭāvatu kaṭṭamāka) putitāka mēlum pala tirāviṭa moḻik kūṟukaḷ cērkkappaṭalāyiṉa. tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar ēṟṟam (Dravidian ascent)  paṟṟiya ik kōṭpāṭāṉatu tol māntar moḻiyiyal muṭivukaḷi mēlum cīrmi peṟac ceyya vallatu; nāḷaṭivil

mānta iṉat (AMH) tōṟṟamum paravalum; moḻip peruṅkuṭumpaṅkaḷ uruvākkamum paravalum; varalāṟṟukku muntiya tolliyal 

ākiya mūṉṟu tuṟikaḷilum aṉittiyum viḷakkattakka oruṅkiṇinta oru peruṅ kōṭpāṭu (Grand synthesis) uruvākka vaḻi kōlak kūṭiyatu ākum.

6. tamatu IJDL cūṉ 2007 kaṭṭuriyil sṭepāṉ leviṭ piṉvarumāṟu “tirāviṭar ēṟṟam” koḷkiyi ātarittuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.



“kaṭal maṭṭam ippoḻutuḷḷati viṭa mikak kuṟivāka irunta paṉiyūḻik kālattil āppirikkā - teṉṉintiyāvi iṇitta vāl pōṉṟa nila iṇippukaḷ / tīvukaḷ vaḻiyāka āp pirikkāviliruntu teṉ āciyāviṟku tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar vantirukkalāmeṉṉum kōṭ pāṭṭi pi.irāmanātaṉ valiyuṟuttukiṟār. ak kōṭpāṭṭi nāṉum ātarikkiṟēṉ. āsti 40 tamiḻar varalāṟu rēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷip paṟṟi āyvuceyta aṟiñar ammakkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷuṭaṉ maṭṭumē toṭarpuḷḷavi eṉak kaṇ ṭuḷḷaṉar. uṟavumuṟi (Kinship) pūmarāṅ (vaḷitaṭi) payaṉpāṭu ākiyaviyum ammakkaḷukkum tirā viṭarukkum uḷḷa toṭarpik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 6000ai oṭṭi ulakeṅkum kaṭal maṭṭam uyarntu nilappakutikaḷiṉ parappu curuṅkiya poḻutu ulakiṉ piṟa pakuti makkaḷuṭiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṭam iruntu tuṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar āstirēliyappaḻaṅ kuṭi makkaḷ. avarkaḷ teṉṉintiyā viliruntu āstirēliyāvukkup pulam peyarntatu ēṟattāḻa 40,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar irukkalām eṉkiṉṟa ṉar āstirēliya aṟiñar. itiliruntu kuṟintatu 40,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē tirāviṭar intiyāvil iruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. inta (“tirāviṭar ēṟṟam”) kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar teṉṉintiyāviliruntu vaṭanāṭu celkiṉṟaṉar; piṉṉar aṅkiruntu pāracīkat tiṟkum atit tāṇṭip piṟa nilappakutikaḷukkum celkiṉṟaṉar. anta piṟa nilappakutikaḷil mūla tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpattiliruntu pirintu urā lik, altāyik, intō-airōppiyam ākiya moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷ uruvākiṉṟaṉa. intō- airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattukku tirāviṭam tāyā? tamakkiyā? eṉpati iṉṟiya niliyil tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟa iyalātu.”

iṉṟiya marapaṇu āyvu “tol tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) - tol āstirēliya moḻi” pēcunar teṉṉintiyāviṟku iṉṟikku 50000 āṇṭu aḷavilēyē vantuviṭṭaṉar eṉpati mēlē 1-ṅa-10il kaṇṭōm. attuṭaṉ niṟuvuvati oppaṉ himar (2003)  The Real Eve : Modern Man’s Journey out of Af r i ca nūliṉpaṭi ma ra paṇu āy vu m (kākacas iṉattic cuṭṭum ā 17) tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar intiyāviliruntu vaṭakku vaṭamēṟkāka ēṟattāḻa 40000 āṇṭukku muṉṉar miya kiḻakku - miya āciyā – airōppā eṉṟavāṟu paṭippaṭiyākap paraviyirukkalām eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭiṟku etirāka illi eṉpatum cuṭṭattakkatu.

iyal 2

cintuveḷit tol tamiḻa nākarikam

ka. tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu (Dravidian Accent) kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyil uruvākiya cintuveḷi nākarikam

1. cintuveḷi nākarikam 1947kku muntiya intiyā, pākistāṉ, vaṅkāḷa tēcam cērnta intiyāviṉ parappaḷavil kāl pakutikku mēl parappaḷavu koṇṭa peru nilap pakutiyil vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil paravi iruntatu. atāvatu cumār 5 laṭcam caturamil parappaḷavil vaḻaṅkiyatu. paṇṭiya mecapotāmiya, ekiptiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ parappiviṭa atika māṉa parappil cintuveḷi nākarikam vaḻaṅkiyatu. an nākarikam vaḻaṅkiya pakutiyil ki.mu. 2000 vākkil mottam aintiliruntu pattu laṭcam makkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉpatu posel, par pōlā pōṉṟavarkaḷ karuttu ākum (appoḻutu ulakil irunta motta makkaḷ tokiyē 9 kōṭikkum kuṟivu). cintu veḷi nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ 1500kkum mēṟpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cumār 20 iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē - mokeñcōtarō, harappā uṭpaṭa - akaḻvāyvukaḷ naṭan tuḷḷaṉa. cintuveḷi nākarikat toṭakkam ki.mu. 7000 liruntu eṉpati meharkār akaḻvāyvu niṟuviyuḷḷatu. an nāka rikattiṉ ciṟappuṟṟa nili ki.mu. 3200-1800 kāla aḷavic cārntatu. mokañcōtarō nakara makkaḷ toki 40,000 eṉṟum harappā nakara makkaḷ toki 25000 eṉṟum matip piṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

2. māntar iṉa (A M H) paraval, moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu (Dravidian Ascent)kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyē cintuveḷi nākarikap pakuti eṉṟu koḷvataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷi mēlē kaṇ ṭōm. annākarikam tirāviṭac cārpuṭiyatu eṉpataṟku uḷḷa mēlum pala valuvāṉa ātāraṅkaḷil cila varumāṟu:-

		i) mokañcōtārō - harappā iṭipāṭukaḷil tirāviṭak kaṭṭumāṉak kaliyik kāṇalām. vīṭukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, naki (āparaṇaṅ)kaḷ, parutti āṭikaḷ aṉittum tirāviṭac cārpuṭiyavi.

		ii) tamiḻariṉ ciṟṟilakka muṟiyiyē cintuveḷi nākarika eṭi aḷavukaḷil kāṇkiṟōm eṉpatic ceṅkam ku. vēṅkaṭācalam niṟuviyuḷḷār.

		iii) iṉṟiya intu matattiṉ mutaṉmik kūṟukaḷ aṉittum tirāviṭa - tamiḻ - cintuveḷi nākarikak kūṟukaḷē ākum. camaṇa, - putta mataṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattukkum cintuveḷi maṟṟum vaṭanāṭṭil paṇṭu irunta tamiḻ (tirāviṭa) ñāṉikaḷē kāraṇamāvar. ikkarutti ṭilar (1973) piṉvarumāṟu kūṟuvār;

iṉṟiya vēta matak karuttukkaḷ – avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa camaṇa puttam mutaliya karuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟiṟku iṭiyē mutalil uṟaḻvu, piṉṉar iṇippu eṉṟavāṟu intumatam uruvāṉatu eṉṟu karutuvatu avvaḷavu cariyāṉatalla. cintu veḷi nākarikam cārnta, paṇṭiya tollintiya (tamiḻiya) nākarikattiṉ puttuyirppē, maṟu malarcciyē iṉṟiya intumatam ākum. 
(The Hindu synthesis was less the dialectical reduction of orthodoxy and heterodoxy than the resurgence of the ancient aboriginal Indus civilisation)

maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum (1903,1923,1930) pi.ṭi.cīṉivāca aiyaṅkārum ikkaruttiṉarē.

iv) iṉṟiya icikkali āriyar tamiḻariṭamiruntu kaṟṟatē (ō. kōsvāmi “intiya icivaralāṟu”, 1957.)

cintuveḷi akaḻvāyvukaḷukku neṭuṅkālam muṉṉarē heviṭ (1888). maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (1903) pōṉṟa nuṇ māṇuḻi pulamikka aṟiñar āriyar varukikku muṉṉar intiyā muḻuvatum vāḻntu vanta nākarikamikka tamiḻiya makkaḷiṭamiruntē āriyar nākarikam peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttit teḷivākak kūṟiyiruntaṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ virivi pi. irāmanātaṉ : cintu veḷit tol tamiḻa nākarikam 1999 iyalkaḷ 3-6 maṟṟum 8 il kāṇka).

3. speyiṉ nāṭṭic cārnta tirāviṭar eṉṟu taṉṉi aḻittuk koṇṭa es. hīrās pātiriyār (11.9.1888 - 14. 12. 1955) toṉmi intō - naṇṇilakkari nākarika āyvu (Studies in Proto - Indo Mediterranean Culture 1953) eṉṉum nūlil cintu veḷi nākarikam tirāviṭaruṭiyatu; avarkaḷ moḻi tirāviṭa moḻi eṉpati niṟuviyuḷḷār. mikap paḻaṅ kālattil (ki.mu. 5000kkum neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉar) mutaṟkaṇ kāvirik kariyil uruvākiya arappā nākarikam intiya mēṟkukkari vaḻiyākat tamiḻarkaḷāl cintuveḷi, cumēriyā, ekiptu naṇṇilakkari nāṭukaḷ vari koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu eṉpatu hīrās koḷki (avar vātat tukku ātāramākak koṇṭa ceytikaḷ cila ippoḻutu tavaṟākat tōṉṟalām; cintuveḷi muttirikaḷi avar tirāviṭa moḻi cārntavi eṉṟu koṇṭa muṭivu cari yeṉṟālum avar muttirikaḷil paṭittuk kaṇṭa vācakaṅkaḷ iṉṟu ēṟkattakkaṉavāka illi. iruntālum avar kaṇṭa aṭippaṭi uṇmi iṉṟum valuvuṭiyatākavē uḷḷatu.)

4. eṉ lākōvari 1963il veḷiyiṭṭa tirāviṭar tōṟṟamum mēl nāṭukaḷum (Dravidian Origins and the West) eṉṉum nūl ciṟanta moḻiyiyal meymmikaḷi uṇarttu vatākum. “tirāviṭar naṇṇilakkari nāṭukaḷiliruntu pulam peyarntu vantu intiyāvil cintuveḷiyil mutaṟkaṇ kuṭiyēṟip piṉṉar kumari vari paraviṉar” eṉṟa apattakkoḷ kiyi avar (vivaram puriyāmal) piṉpaṟṟiyirunta pōtilum avar niṟuviya piṉvarum meymmikaḷ mukkiyamāṉavi: 

i) 6000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar intiyāviliruntu aṭlāṇṭik kaṭalvari orē mātiriyāṉa “pala col piṇippu oṭṭunili” (Polysynthetic Suffixal) moḻikaḷ iṭiyīṭu iṉṟip paraviyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ coṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ ōraḷavukku oṉṟupōl iruntaṉa. potuc col viḻukkāṭu moḻikku moḻi māṟupaṭṭu iruntirukkalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, elāmiṭ, cumēriyaṉ, harri, kākēciyaṉ, halṭi, pōṉṟavi orē moḻiyamippuk koṇṭu oṉṟukkoṉṟu uṟavuṭiyaviyākac caṅkilit toṭarpōla amintiruntaṉa.

ii) intō airōppiya moḻi pēcuvōr (ki.mu 2000ai oṭṭi) airōppāvukkuḷ kiḻakkiliruntu nuḻiyumuṉṉar aṅku pēcappaṭṭu vanta moḻikaḷil oṉṟāṉa pāsku (yūskarā) iṉṟum pirāṉcukkum speyiṉukkum iṭippaṭṭa pireṉīs malippakutiyil pēcappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. tol tirāviṭa moḻi inta pāsku moḻiyuṭaṉ maṭṭumaṉṟi intō airōppiya moḻikaḷukku muṉṉar airōppāvil pēcappaṭṭa toṉ moḻikaḷuṭaṉum uṟavuṭiyatu.

iii) immoḻikaḷ aṉittum oṉṟukkoṉṟu neruṅkiya toṭarpuḷḷavi; avi potuvāṉa orē tāy moḻi yiṉ vaṭṭāra vaḻakkukaḷ eṉṟē kūṟalām.

5. cintuveḷi nākarikam intō āriya moḻi pēcunar uruvākkiyatāka irukkalām eṉa iṉṟiya intiya aṟiñar (kuṟippāka vaṭanāṭṭaṟiñar) cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itu tavaṟu eṉpatic caṟṟē viḷakkuvōm. vēta moḻiyākiya intō airōppiya moḻi intiyāvil uruvākiyirukkalām eṉṟu vēta, camaskirutappaṟṟāḷar kūṟuvatu apattam [ituvēṟu; ki.mu. 10000kku muṉṉar toltirāviṭamoḻiyiliruntu kiḷitta moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaḷuḷ intō airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpamum oṉṟu eṉṟa ñāṉappirakācar – tēvanēyaṉ kōṭpāṭu vēṟu!] ki.mu. 1700 - 1500 kālakaṭṭattil intiyā viṟkuḷ vaṭamēṟkiliruntu nuḻintavarkaḷ vētamoḻi (Vedic Language)ākiya intō āriya moḻi pēcunar āvar. ivar kaḷ cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷōṭu oppiṭum pōtu ciṟu eṇṇikkiyiṉarākavē iruntirukka vēṇṭum. intō airōppiya moḻip peruṅkuṭumpattil aṭaṅkiyavi intō āriya moḻiyākiya vētamoḻi tavira irāṉiyaṉ (avestaṉ), aṉaṭōliyaṉ (hiṭṭiṭ), armīṉiyaṉ, ṭōkkāriyaṉ, alpēṉiyaṉ, kirīkku, ittālik (ilattīṉ mutaliyavi), kelṭik (airiṣ, velṣ uṭpaṭa), jermāṉik (āṅkilam uṭpaṭa), pālṭik (laṭviyaṉ, littuvēṉiyaṉ), slāv (raṣyaṉmutaliyavi) ākiyaviyākum. intap pal vēṟu intō airōppiya moḻi pēcunarum oruṅku cērntu ki.mu. 4000 - 3000 kāla aḷavil karuṅkaṭal - kāspiyaṉ kaṭal pakutikku vaṭakkil mattiya āciya - airōppiya sṭeppi pulveḷikaḷil vacittu vanta nāṭōṭikaḷ (nomads)āvar. ak kālakkaṭṭattil avarkaḷ intō airōppiyat toṉ moḻiyip pēcivantaṉar. ki. mu. 3000 - 2000 kāla aḷavil teṉ kiḻakkākavum nakaralāyiṉar. mēṟkil ceṉṟa nāṭōṭik kuḻuviṉaruḷ oṉṟāṉa kirīkkumoḻi pēcunar nuḻinta pakuti yil (tirāviṭac cārpāṉa) appakutip paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu kirīkku moḻi pēcunar uṭaṉaṭiyāka nākarikam peṟṟaṉar. kiḻakku teṉkiḻakkākap peyarnta nāṭōṭik kuḻuviṉaruḷ intō āriya moḻi pēcum kuḻuvum oṉṟu. ki.mu. 1700 - 1500il avarkaḷ intiyāvukkuḷ irāṉiliruntu nuḻinta poḻutu avarkaḷum cintuveḷit tol nākarikattiṉariṭa miruntu virivil nākarikam peṟṟaṉar. cintuveḷit tirāviṭa aṟiñarkaḷ (avarkaḷil cilar riṣikaḷākavum ēṟkappaṭṭaṉar) paṭippukaḷum nēraṭiyākavō moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭō rik vētattilēyē ēṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (1903, 1923, 1930), vālparṭ pōṉṟōr karuttākum.

6. taṅkaḷuṭiya The rise of civilisation in India and Pakistan  (1988)nūlil piriṭjaṭ maṟṟum rēmāṇṭ ālciṉ tampatiyar. 
karuttum ituvē :

	(The earliest Indo-Aryan text, the compiled Rg Veda, shows several influences of a non-Indo-Aryan, Dravidian element, in the form of phonetic changes, introduction of loan words and names etc.These presuppose the coexistence of the Vedic and Dravidian speaking peoples in a cultural contact situation for a period, perhaps of centuries, before the compilation of the Rg Veda (circa 1500- 1300BC)

mutaṟkaṇ intō āriya moḻi pēcunarukkum cintu veḷi nākarikat tamiḻarukkum (tirāviṭarukkum) iṭiyē vaṉ muṟi ēṟpaṭṭa pōtilum virivil iruvakiyārum kalantu viṭṭaṉar. āriyarāl nakaraṅkaḷ tākkappaṭṭirukkalām. intira ṉiṉ peyarē purantaraṉ (nakaraṅkaḷi aḻippavaṉ) tāṉē! nakaraṅkaḷiṉ vīḻccikku ki.mu. 1700ai oṭṭi nikaḻnta iyaṟki, cuṟṟup puṟac cūḻnilik kāraṇaṅkaḷum ōraḷavu kāraṇamāyirun tirukkalām eṉṟu ālciṉ (1995) kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl cintu veḷi nākarikam paraviyirunta ciṟṟūrppuṟap perunilap parappil (ciṟu eṇṇikkiyil nuḻinta) āriyarāl peruntāk kam ēṟpaṭuttiyirukka muṭiyātu. ākavētāṉ cunītikumār cāṭṭarji, vālṭar pērcarvīs, es.ē.ṭilar pōṉṟōr kūṟuvatupōla iṉṟiya nākarikam, paṇpāṭu, intumatam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭittaḷam (rūpāykku 12 aṇā aḷavukku eṉpār caṭṭarji) tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi, paṇpāṭu āki yaviyē. intō āriya moḻi pēcunar paraval paṟṟiya varalāṟṟiliruntē avarkaḷ ki.mu. 1700kku muntiya cintu nākarikatti uruvākkiyirukka iyalātu eṉpatu teriyum. cintu veḷi nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil āriyarukku nerukkamāṉa kutiri kiṭiyātu eṉpatum mukkiyamāṉatu.

7. intō āriya moḻiyiṉ rikvēta niliyilēyē nuḻintuḷḷa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷāka cumār nāṟpatu – aimpatu coṟkaḷi parō, emaṉō pōṉṟavarkaḷ ēṟkeṉavē aṭi yāḷam kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal - ōr aṟi mukam (1990) nūlil itik kuṟippiṭum cuvelapil, rik vētak kālattiliruntē intō āriya moḻi kaṭaṉ peṟṟuḷḷa ēṟattāḻa 10 moḻiyiyaṟ kūṟukaḷiyum paṭṭiyaliṭṭuḷḷār. [iviyellām vēta camaṟkiruta moḻiyil tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ tākkam eṉṟa aḷaviltāṉ ivarkaḷ āyvukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl ñāṉap pirakācarum pāvāṇarum kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷ ivaṟṟiviṭa mika viñciyavi. avviruvarum “intō - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi vērc coṟkaḷ palavum tol - tirāviṭa moḻiyiliruntu peṟṟaviyē” eṉṟa karuttuṭiyavarkaḷ.]

8. cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟum an nākarika muttiri eḻuttukkaḷ tol tamiḻē eṉṟum takuti vāynta aṟiñar aṉivarum ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. Indus Script Dravidian (1995) nūlil irā. mativāṇaṉ, cintu veḷiyil muntu tamiḻ (2004) nūlil pūrṇa cantira jīvā ākiyōr av veḻuttukkaḷit toltamiḻākap paṭikkum poḻutu tol kāppiyattiliruntu kiṭṭum moḻiyiyal taravukaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. puṟanāṉūṟu 201, 202il kuṟippiṭappaṭum iruṅkōvēḷiṉ muṉṉōr cintuveḷi nākarikattōṭu toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷākak kapilarāl karutappaṭṭaṉar. puṟanāṉūṟu 202 kuṟippiṭum ariyam rikvētam 6.27. 5il cuṭṭappaṭum hariyūpiyā tāṉ eṉpatu pi.el. cāmi karuttu (centamiḻc celvi caṉavari 1994). ariyam = aracamaram. ariya + akappā = ariyakappā = harappā eṉṟu peyar māṟiyatu eṉkiṟār avar. iruṅkōvēḷiṉ muṉṉōr 49 talimuṟikaḷukku muṉṉar (atāvatu tol paḻaṅkālattil) pukaḻpeṟṟa tuvari eṉṉum kōṭṭi nakari āṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu puṟam 201 kūṟukiṟatu. tamatu tolkāppiyap pāyira vuriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vēḷir tuvārapati (tuvāraki)yiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉkiṉṟār. ariyam harappāvittāṉ kuṟippatākak koṇṭāl harappā paṟṟiyum tuvāraki paṟṟiyum kapilar kālattil vaḻaṅkiya (oru nakarukkuriyati maṟṟataṟkuriyatāka māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiya) toṉmak karuttip puṟam 201, 202 pāṭalkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉka. itaṉi jīvā mēlum āyvu ceykiṟār. vēḷ-vēṭ-pēṭ (Bet) - pēṭ tuvārakā eṉṟu tuvārakiyiṉ peyar varalāṟṟi avar tarukiṟār.

9. cumēriya nākarika muttirikaḷil ciṅkaṅkaḷ iraṇṭik kollum kilkāmeṣ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kilkāmeṣ paṟṟiya toṉmak katiyum cumēriyap poṟippukaḷil tarap paṭukiṟatu. cintuveḷi muttirikaḷ iraṇṭil (kilkāmēṣ ciṅkaṅkaḷit tākkuvatu pōlavē) intiya vīraṉ oruvaṉ pulikaḷ iraṇṭi kikku oṉṟākak kolvatu pōl cittirikkappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ. puṟam 201 iruṅkōvēḷip ‘puli
kaṭimāl’ eṉṟu aḻikkiṟatu. iruṅkōvēḷiṉ muṉṉōr kālam cintuveḷi nākarikak kālattiliruntu toṭaṅki yirukkalām eṉa ai. makātēvaṉ Journal of Tamil Studies mē
1970 itaḻil uṉṉittiruntār. āyiṉum 2002 caṉavariyil avar karuttu anta uṉṉippiṟku valuvāṉa ātāram illi eṉpatākum.

10. rikvētam ai. 133, 1 & 3iṉ āṅkila vaṭivam varumāṟu;

		“ō makavāṉ! aḻintupaṭṭa vilastāṉaka nakarattilum,
		aḻintupaṭṭa makāvilasta nakarattilum uḷḷa cūṉiyak
		kārikaḷ kumpalkaḷi aḻittu ōḻippāyāka”

		“nāṉ mēlulakattiyum pūmiyiyum cattiyattiṉāl
		tūymippaṭuttukiṟēṉ. vilastāṉa nakaril intiraṉāl
		tōṟkaṭikkappaṭṭu koliyuṇṭu kiṭakkum (intiraṉi
		eritta) āṟṟal mikka tuṣṭap picācukaḷi nāṉ erittu
		oḻikkiṟēṉ”

āriyarkaḷāl nācamākkappaṭṭa nakarattiṉ peyarāṉa ‘vilas tāṉam’ eṉpatu āriyamoḻiyallāta piṟamoḻic collāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu ṭākṭar parō karuttu ākum. (jarṉal āp intiyaṉ hisṭari XII - 1 ēpral 1963,) orukkāl ‘vilastāṉam’ eṉpatu vēḷiruṭiya ūr/nakarik kuṟittirukkalām.

11. cintu mutaliya āṟukaḷil cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷ kaṭṭiyirunta aṇikaḷi āriyar uṭittu nāṭṭi veḷḷak kāṭākki aḻittirukkalām eṉpar tāmōtar tarmāṉant kōcampi (The culture and Civilisation of Ancient India in historical
outline 1974). maturik kāñci 725ām aṭiyilum aka nāṉūṟu 346ām pāṭalilum āṟṟiṉ kuṟukkē kaṭṭappaṭum aṇikaḷik kuṟikka ‘kaṟciṟi’ eṉṉum col payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. rik vētattil aṇiyik kuṟippiṭum cirā (Sira) eṉṉum col ‘kaṟciṟiyiṉ’ citivē eṉpar pi.el. cāmi (centamiḻc celvi : 1994 navampar).


akaḻvāyvil kiṭittuḷḷa mukkiyamāṉa
cintuveḷi nākarikat talaṅkaḷ



u. cintuveḷi nākarika muttiri eḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻiya
moḻiyākap paṭikkattakkaviyē eṉpatum tamiḻ vari
vaṭivam cintuveḷi varivaṭivattiliruntē
uruvākiyirukkalām eṉpatum

1. cintuveḷi nākarika muttiri eḻuttukkaḷ eṉpavi mūvāyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭa “sṭīyaṭṭiṭ” (cavarkkārakkal, atāvatu mākkal) muttirikaḷilum cila cepput takaṭukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟilum uḷḷavi. ovvoru muttiriyum cumār 20 mi.mī. x 30 mi.mī. aḷavuḷḷatu. cila caturamāṉavi. perum pālaṉavaṟṟil oru vilaṅkiṉ uruvamum ataṉ mēl pakkattil oṉṟiliruntu pattup paṉṉiraṇṭu (carācari 5) kuṟiyīṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. mika nīṇṭa toṭar 26 kuṟiyīṭukaḷ koṇṭatu. cintu veḷiyiliruntu paruttit tuṇi pōṉṟavaṟṟi cumēriyā pōṉṟa mēlināṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyumpoḻutu
cippaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi avaṟṟiṉmēl poruḷukkuriyavar peyarik kaḷimaṇṇil muttiriyiṭa im muttirikaḷuḷ perum pālāṉavi payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatē ciṟanta āyvaṟiñar kaḷ karuttu. vīlar, haṇṭar, kāṭ (Gadd), kōcampi, kō (Coe) ākiyōr inta aṭippaṭiyiltāṉ muttirik kuṟiyīṭukaḷil uḷḷavi taṉi āṭkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷākat tāṉ (cila nērvu kaḷil paṭṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ) irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivukku vantuḷḷaṉar. cippattik kaṭṭiya kayiṟu, pāy ivaṟṟiṉ cuvaṭukaḷ cila kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (cila kaṭṭikaḷ orō vaḻi neruppiṉ vāyppāṭṭu cuṭappaṭṭatāl avi maṭṭumē iṉṟu kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiltāṉ iv vaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa) āka, ālciṉ (1988) kūṟuvatu pōl “im muttirikaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭu (allatu payaṉpāṭu kaḷil oṉṟu) vāṇika naṭavaṭikkikaḷ cārntatāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli” (There can be little doubt that
the Harappan seals were - atleast as one of their functions – necessary elements in the mechanism of trade. p. 185.)cila muttirikaḷ tāyat tukkaḷākavum nērttik kaṭaṉ villikaḷākavum aṭiyāḷa ilacciṉikaḷākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu irukkalām.

2. pala muttirikaḷil oṟṟik kompu teriyum erutu oṉṟiṉ muṉṉar eṉṟa vaṭivam uḷḷatu. makātēvaṉ karuttu (1985, 1984) ivvaṭivam cōmaccāṟu vaṭikkappaṭṭa ēṉam; piṉṉar cōmaccāṟu āriyarkaḷālum mukkiyamāṉatāka ēṟkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum. piṟkālattil ivvaṭivam intirat vajam āṉatu. aḷakkuṭi cīttārāmaṉ karūril kaṇṭeṭutta ceppu muttiriyilum inta vaṭivam uḷḷatu eṉpatu makā tēvaṉ karuttu ākum.

3. cintuveḷi eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ēṟattāḻa 400 aḷavil uḷḷaṉa. (parpōlā 385, makātēvaṉ 417). 2906 muttiri vācakaṅkaḷil kaṇṭa kuṟiyīṭukaḷi makātēvaṉ piṉvarumāṟu vakippaṭuttukiṟār:

kuṟiyīṭu 		ovvoṉṟum 		mottam 		mottattil
				ettaṉi 			avi 		viḻukkāṭu
				taṭavi 			ettaṉi
								taṭavi
								varukiṉṟaṉa

1 			100m ataṟku mēlum 	1395 			10.43
